### 16.3 Output Artifacts

QSASE should create:

```text
data/runtime/qsase_component_attribution_ledger.json
data/runtime/qsase_component_attribution_ledger.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_component_attribution_ledger_history.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_component_attribution_ledger_events.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_strategy_weight_proposals.json
data/runtime/qsase_source_trust_proposals.json
data/runtime/qsase_model_weight_proposals.json
data/runtime/qsase_filter_threshold_proposals.json
data/runtime/qsase_learning_approval_queue.json
```

The summary artifact should expose:

```text
status
generated_at
attribution_record_count
real_paper_lifecycle_record_count
non_trade_record_count
shadow_replay_record_count
backtest_record_count
rejected_hypothesis_record_count
blocked_route_record_count
system_defect_record_count
strategy_weight_proposal_count
source_trust_proposal_count
model_weight_proposal_count
filter_threshold_proposal_count
approval_required_count
approved_proposal_count
applied_update_count
learning_write_created
strategy_mutation_created
policy_mutation_created
model_weight_update_created
trust_score_update_created
paper_order_created_count
broker_write_count
proof_credit_allowed
live_capital_enabled
```

`applied_update_count`, `learning_write_created`, `strategy_mutation_created`,
`policy_mutation_created`, `model_weight_update_created`,
`trust_score_update_created`, `paper_order_created_count`,
`broker_write_count`, `proof_credit_allowed`, and `live_capital_enabled` must
remain zero or false unless a future explicitly approved governance path is
implemented. The first QSASE implementation should keep them zero or false.

### 16.4 Attribution Record Contract

Each attribution record should use a schema like:

```json
{
  "attribution_record_id": "qsase-attribution-energy-event-probability-lag-001",
  "status": "recorded_proposal_only",
  "evidence_class": "real_paper_lifecycle",
  "generated_at": "2026-06-28T12:00:00Z",
  "lineage": {
    "strategy_hypothesis_id": "qsase-strategy-energy-event-probability-lag-001",
    "router_decision_id": "qsase-router-energy-event-probability-lag-001",
    "paperops_handoff_id": "qsase-paperops-handoff-energy-event-probability-lag-001",
    "paper_lifecycle_ref": "data/runtime/paper_lifecycle_portfolio_postmortem.json",
    "paper_proof_ledger_ref": "data/runtime/paper_proof_ledger.json",
    "research_goal_id": "rg-energy-event-probability-lag-001"
  },
  "decision_context": {
    "strategy_family": "energy_event_probability_lag",
    "instrument": "XLE",
    "direction": "long",
    "horizon": "3d_to_10d",
    "market_regime": "event_risk_rising_with_energy_confirmation",
    "decision_state": "paper_submitted_through_guarded_route"
  },
  "outcome_summary": {
    "outcome_state": "closed_paper_trade",
    "return_pct": 1.4,
    "max_adverse_excursion_pct": -0.6,
    "max_favorable_excursion_pct": 2.1,
    "invalidation_hit": false,

  },

    },

    },

    },
    "quantum_review": {
      "contribution": "helped",
      "reason": "Quantum review kept the nonlinear event-probability lag under review without granting trade authority."
    },
    "akber_filter": {

    },

    },
    "paperops_gate": {
      "contribution": "helped",
      "reason": "Gate preserved idempotency, duplicate exposure, and guarded route checks."
    },
    "risk_and_execution": {
      "contribution": "helped",
      "reason": "Sizing and route stayed within paper risk limits."
    }
  },
  "causal_assessment": {
    "primary_driver": "source_price_event_lag",
    "secondary_driver": "akber_market_confirmation",
    "suspected_luck_component": "low",

    "overfit_risk": "low_to_medium",
    "confidence": 0.66
  },
  "proposal": {
    "proposal_type": "strategy_weight_proposal",
    "proposal_state": "ready_pending_learning_approval",
    "target": "energy_event_probability_lag",

    "reason": "Real paper outcome and shadow replay both support a small weight increase.",
    "apply_allowed": false,
    "applied": false
  },
  "authority": {
    "learning_write_created": false,
    "strategy_mutation_created": false,
    "policy_mutation_created": false,
    "model_weight_update_created": false,
    "trust_score_update_created": false,
    "paper_order_created": false,

```

The key design point is that the record can contain a proposal, but the
proposal is not an applied change.

### 16.5 Evidence Classes

QSASE should separate evidence by class.

`real_paper_lifecycle`:

```text
A real paper setup entered the guarded PaperOps lifecycle and produced an
observable paper outcome.
```

This is the strongest evidence class for strategy learning, but it still does
not automatically grant proof credit or mutate strategy weights.

`non_trade_decision`:

```text
QSASE, PaperOps, or the router decided not to trade, and the later market path
can be evaluated against that decision.
```

This is crucial. A high-quality no-trade decision can be a positive learning
event. A blocked bad setup is not inactivity; it is discipline.

`shadow_replay`:

```text
A strategy ran forward or historically without placing a paper order.
```

This can support strategy research and proposal generation, but it cannot
support paper proof ledger credit.

`backtest_observation`:

```text
A historical source-price pattern was replayed against historical prices.
```

This can support hypothesis formation and overfit testing, but it must remain
separate from current paper lifecycle evidence.

`rejected_hypothesis`:

```text
A Strategy Foundry hypothesis was rejected before routing or before PaperOps.
```

Rejected hypotheses should be learned from instead of discarded silently. A
rejected idea may reveal source noise, overfitting, missing market expression,
or a future data-source gap.

`blocked_route`:

```text
A strategy looked plausible, but route state, account mirror state, source
coverage, idempotency, drawdown, Q-CTRL hold, or system health prevented
review or submission.
```

Blocked-route records should distinguish valid safety holds from system
defects.

`system_defect`:

```text
The learning system could not evaluate the decision because an artifact,
schema, mirror, route, or check was missing, stale, or contradictory.
```

System defects should feed self-healing, not strategy-weight changes.

### 16.6 Component Attribution Model

Every attribution record should score each major component as one of:

```text
helped
hurt
neutral
unknown
blocked
not_applicable
```

The component map should include:

- Source Universe.
- Source quorum.
- Historical Source-Price Memory.
- Universal Source-Price Pattern Matrix.
- Linear Pattern Recognition Lab.
- Nonlinear And Quantum Pattern Lab.
- Local Gemma model.
- Frontier Gemini model.
- IBM quantum gates oracle.
- Akber Filter.
- Strategy Foundry.
- Strategy Router.
- PaperOps Gate Interface.
- Signal Integrity.
- Strategy Lead.
- Head of Quant review where required.
- Risk Agent.
- Execution Policy.
- Idempotency ledger.
- Duplicate exposure check.
- Daily drawdown check.
- Q-CTRL paper consultation hold.
- Guarded Alpaca Paper route.
- Paper lifecycle poller.
- Self-healing repair loop.

The point is not to pretend exact causal attribution is always possible. The
point is to avoid generic learning statements such as "strategy improved" and
instead say exactly which part of Qadam's stack improved or failed.

### 16.7 Causal Assessment

The ledger should classify why an outcome happened.

Possible labels:

- `worked_for_expected_reason`
- `worked_for_unexpected_reason`
- `failed_for_expected_reason`
- `failed_for_unexpected_reason`
- `blocked_correctly`
- `blocked_but_opportunity_missed`
- `rejected_correctly`
- `rejected_but_pattern_later_confirmed`
- `route_defect_prevented_evaluation`
- `source_noise_dominated`
- `regime_bias_dominated`
- `execution_luck_dominated`
- `overfit_likely`
- `insufficient_evidence`

These labels should be backed by source-price evidence, not model prose alone.
For example:

```text
If a paper trade made money but the source pattern reversed immediately after
entry, the ledger should not mark the strategy as clean edge.
```

Similarly:

```text
If a setup was rejected by Akber's filter and the market later failed to
confirm, the ledger should credit the filter even though no trade occurred.
```

### 16.8 Strategy Weight Proposals

QSASE may propose strategy-weight changes.

It must not apply them directly.

Proposal types should include:

- `increase_strategy_weight`
- `decrease_strategy_weight`
- `hold_strategy_weight`
- `retire_strategy_variant`
- `split_strategy_family`
- `merge_strategy_families`
- `increase_source_trust`
- `decrease_source_trust`
- `hold_source_trust`
- `increase_model_weight`
- `decrease_model_weight`
- `hold_model_weight`
- `tighten_akber_filter`
- `loosen_akber_filter`
- `require_quantum_review`
- `reduce_quantum_review_priority`
- `router_policy_review`
- `paperops_gate_precheck_review`
- `source_coverage_repair_request`

Each proposal should include:

```text
proposal_id
proposal_type
proposal_state
target_surface
target_id
current_value
proposed_value
suggested_delta
evidence_refs
supporting_record_count
contradicting_record_count
real_paper_evidence_count
shadow_evidence_count
backtest_evidence_count
overfit_risk
source_noise_risk
regime_bias_risk
expected_effect
approval_required
apply_allowed
applied
```

`apply_allowed` and `applied` must be false in the QSASE ledger output.

### 16.9 Approval Boundary

The current Qadam learning architecture already treats learning outputs as
approval-gated proposals. QSASE should continue that pattern.

The ledger may:

- Record attribution.
- Record causal assessment.
- Record proposal candidates.
- Queue learning approval review.
- Explain whether a proposal is supported, weak, blocked, or contradictory.
- Feed dashboard decision records.
- Feed short Telegram learning notes.
- Feed self-healing repair requests.

The ledger must not:

- Apply strategy weights.
- Apply model weights.
- Apply trust-score updates.
- Mutate active strategy policy.
- Mutate source artifacts.
- Create knowledge graph writes unless an explicit approved staging path exists.
- Create PaperOps qualified setups.
- Create paper orders.
- Create broker writes.
- Grant proof credit.
- Enable live capital.

The correct default is:

```text
proposal_recorded_not_applied
```

If an approval path is added later, it must be a separate explicit module with
its own audit record, not an implicit side effect of attribution.

### 16.10 Relationship To The Paper Proof Ledger

The paper proof ledger and the Learning And Attribution Ledger have different
jobs.

The paper proof ledger records real paper lifecycle evidence for the 30-day
paper growth trial.

The Learning And Attribution Ledger explains what QSASE thinks caused those
outcomes.

Rules:

- The attribution ledger may reference paper proof ledger records.
- The attribution ledger may explain paper proof ledger outcomes.
- The attribution ledger may propose future strategy changes from those
  outcomes.
- The attribution ledger must not create paper proof ledger entries.
- The attribution ledger must not grant paper proof ledger credit.
- The attribution ledger must not convert shadow replay or backtests into paper
  proof ledger evidence.
- The attribution ledger must not simulate elapsed time in the 30-day paper
  growth trial.

This separation prevents QSASE from marking its own homework.

### 16.11 Non-Trade Learning

QSASE should learn from non-trades as first-class events.

A non-trade record should capture:

```text
why_not_trading_now_reason
candidate_count
held_candidate_count
rejected_candidate_count
top_missing_evidence
source_quorum_state
akber_filter_state
quantum_review_state
router_state
paperops_gate_state
risk_state
route_state
later_market_confirmation_state
missed_opportunity_assessment
discipline_credit_assessment
```

Examples:

```text
No trade because source quorum failed. Later market action was noisy and did
not confirm. Attribution: source quorum helped.
```

```text
No trade because paper account mirror was stale. Later market action confirmed
the setup. Attribution: route defect blocked evaluation, not strategy failure.
Self-healing repair requested.
```

This is how QSASE avoids confusing safety, discipline, and infrastructure
defects with lack of strategy edge.

### 16.12 Shadow And Backtest Learning

Shadow and backtest evidence should influence research confidence, not proof.

For every shadow or backtest learning record, QSASE should store:

```text
simulation_type
historical_window
data_sources_used
instruments_evaluated
source_price_pattern_id
strategy_hypothesis_id
walk_forward_split
out_of_sample_result
overfit_tests
nonlinear_pattern_tests
quantum_review_ref
paperability_assessment
paper_lifecycle_evidence_count
proof_credit_allowed
```

`proof_credit_allowed` must be false for all shadow and backtest records.

Shadow and backtest records can create or strengthen a proposal such as:

```text
Strategy family should remain in shadow until it produces three clean
out-of-sample confirmations and one paper-review eligible setup.
```

They cannot create:

- A paper order.
- A real paper trade.
- A paper proof ledger entry.
- A proof claim.

### 16.13 System Defect Attribution

The ledger must prevent one common failure mode:

```text
Qadam did not trade because the system was broken, but the dashboard described
it as strategy discipline.
```

System defects should receive their own attribution path.

Examples:

- Missing runtime artifact.
- Stale paper account mirror.
- Broken route summary.
- Missing idempotency ledger.
- Missing Q-CTRL state.
- Contradictory live-capital flag.
- Invalid source matrix schema.
- Missing strategy-router decision.
- PaperOps check failure.
- Telegram notification failure.
- Dashboard mirror stale.

For these records, the ledger should write:

```text
evidence_class = system_defect
strategy_weight_proposal_count = 0
self_healing_repair_requested = true
```

The system should fix infrastructure before drawing strategy conclusions.

### 16.14 Dashboard Representation

The dashboard should represent the learning ledger as decision records, not
essays.

Recommended module:

```text
Learning Ledger
Latest outcome: no trade
Cause: Akber filter held weak confirmation
Later market check: no durable confirmation
Attribution: filter helped
Proposal: hold filter threshold
Applied: no
```

Another example:

```text
Learning Ledger
Latest outcome: blocked route
Cause: paper account mirror stale
Attribution: system defect, not strategy failure
Proposal: repair mirror refresh
Applied: no
```

Recommended fields:

- Latest attribution state.
- Latest evidence class.
- Real paper evidence count.
- Shadow evidence count.
- Backtest evidence count.
- Non-trade learning count.
- Rejected hypothesis learning count.
- Top credited component.
- Top blamed component.
- Active proposal count.
- Approval-required count.
- Applied update count.
- Proof boundary.
- Live-capital boundary.

The dashboard should never say "Qadam learned" without saying what evidence
supports the learning and whether anything was actually applied.

### 16.15 Telegram Representation

Telegram learning messages should be short, specific, and review-only.

Allowed message shape:

```text
Qadam learning note
Outcome: no trade
Cause: Akber filter held weak confirmation
Later check: market did not confirm
Proposal: keep filter strict
Applied: no
```

For route defects:

```text
Qadam repair note
Outcome: setup not evaluated
Cause: paper mirror stale
Next: repair queued
No trade submitted
```

Telegram must not announce broad self-improvement claims, strategy mutations,
approval language, broker instructions, or live-capital authority.

### 16.16 Relationship To Recursive Improvement

The Learning And Attribution Ledger feeds the Recursive Improvement Contract in
Part 17.

It provides:

- Evidence-separated attribution.
- Proposal candidates.
- System defect repair requests.
- Strategy-family performance notes.
- Source-trust notes.
- Model-weight notes.
- Akber filter notes.
- Quantum review usefulness notes.
- Router correctness notes.
- PaperOps gate usefulness notes.

Part 17 should consume these records and decide how recursive improvement is
allowed to proceed.

The ledger should not itself decide:

- Which changes become active.
- Which strategy becomes dominant.
- Which model gets more authority.
- Which source becomes trusted.
- Which PaperOps gate changes.
- Which proof standard changes.

It records the evidence and proposes the review item. Governance decides what
changes.

### 16.17 Build Contract

Implementation should add:

```text
orchestrator/qsase_learning_attribution_ledger.py
scripts/check_qsase_learning_attribution_ledger.py
```

The module should expose:

```text
build_learning_attribution_ledger(settings)
load_learning_attribution_ledger(settings)
validate_learning_attribution_ledger(payload)
write_learning_attribution_ledger(payload, settings)
build_component_attribution_records(context)
build_non_trade_attribution_records(context)
build_shadow_attribution_records(context)
build_backtest_attribution_records(context)
build_system_defect_attribution_records(context)
build_strategy_weight_proposals(attribution_records)
build_source_trust_proposals(attribution_records)
build_model_weight_proposals(attribution_records)
build_filter_threshold_proposals(attribution_records)
build_learning_approval_queue(proposals)
```

The check script should validate:

- Required input artifacts exist or missing inputs are explicitly reported.
- Evidence class is present for every record.
- Real paper lifecycle evidence is separated from shadow and backtest evidence.
- Non-trade decisions are first-class records.
- Rejected hypotheses are first-class records.
- System defects do not create strategy-weight proposals.
- Every proposal has evidence references.
- Every proposal is approval-required.
- No proposal is applied by this ledger.
- `apply_allowed` is false.
- `applied_update_count` is zero.
- Strategy mutation is false.
- Policy mutation is false.
- Model-weight update creation is false.
- Trust-score update creation is false.
- Learning write creation is false unless a future approved staging path exists.
- Paper order creation is zero.
- Broker write count is zero.
- Proof credit is false.
- Live capital is false.
- Dashboard summary is decision-record based.
- Telegram summary is short and command-disabled.

The first implementation should be conservative. It should record attribution
and proposals even when evidence is thin, but it should mark weak evidence as
weak instead of forcing a conclusion.

### 16.18 Learning And Attribution Ledger Summary

The Learning And Attribution Ledger is where QSASE becomes self-aware in the
engineering sense: it reflects on its own source inputs, model reasoning,
quantum reviews, filters, routing decisions, PaperOps gates, non-trades,
paper outcomes, and route defects.

It does not treat profit as proof. It does not treat inactivity as failure. It
does not treat a model narrative as learning. It asks whether Qadam was right,
whether it was right for the expected reason, which component caused the
result, and what should be reviewed next.

Most importantly, it keeps learning proposal-first. QSASE can attribute,
explain, and recommend. It cannot silently mutate strategy, source trust, model
weights, PaperOps gates, proof credit, broker state, or live-capital authority.

## 17. Recursive Improvement Contract

The Recursive Improvement Contract defines how QSASE gets better over time
without confusing improvement with uncontrolled autonomy.

It asks:

```text
How does Qadam repeatedly improve its source coverage, pattern recognition,
strategy hypotheses, routing, filters, paper readiness, and learning proposals
without silently changing its own trading authority?
```

QSASE should be recursive in the engineering sense: it observes its own inputs,
outputs, failures, and outcomes; compares them against later evidence; proposes
specific improvements; routes system defects to repair; and repeats the cycle.
It is not recursive in the sense of self-granting permissions, bypassing gates,
or treating model confidence as authority.

### 17.1 Purpose

The purpose of the Recursive Improvement Contract is to define the safe loop
that turns Qadam from a static trading system into an adaptive research and
paper-operation organism.

It should answer:

```text
What did QSASE observe?
What pattern did it discover?
How was the pattern tested?
Was the test linear, nonlinear, quantum-reviewed, or full-universe?
Was the strategy hypothesis paperable?
Did the router handle it correctly?
Did PaperOps accept, hold, reject, or submit it?
What happened after the decision?
What did the Learning And Attribution Ledger conclude?
What improvement was proposed?
Was the proposal approved?
Was any system defect repaired?
What should the next cycle do differently?
```

This is the contract that prevents QSASE from becoming a pile of interesting
research artifacts. Every insight should either:

- strengthen a strategy hypothesis,
- weaken a strategy hypothesis,
- create a new hypothesis,
- reject a hypothesis,
- propose a governed change,
- request a repair,
- or explicitly record that there is not enough evidence.

### 17.2 Core Principle

The core principle is:

```text
recursive improvement is allowed;
silent mutation is not.
```

QSASE may recursively improve:

- source coverage maps,
- source-price pattern candidates,
- historical memory quality,
- backtest diagnostics,
- shadow strategy diagnostics,
- strategy hypotheses,
- strategy-family definitions,
- router recommendations,
- Akber filter recommendations,
- quantum review priority recommendations,
- dashboard explanations,
- Telegram summaries,
- repair requests,
- learning proposals.

QSASE must not recursively mutate:

- active strategy weights,
- model weights,
- source trust scores,
- PaperOps gates,
- risk limits,
- execution policy,
- broker route authority,
- paper proof ledger credit,
- live-capital state.

All mutations remain outside this contract unless a separate governance module
approves and records them.

### 17.3 The Full Recursive Loop

The QSASE recursive loop should run in this order:

```text
1. Self-model refresh
2. Universal source-price matrix refresh
3. Historical source-price memory refresh
4. Full-universe pattern search
5. Linear pattern recognition lab
6. Nonlinear and quantum pattern lab
7. Strategy Foundry hypothesis generation
8. Akber Filter review
9. Shadow Strategy Simulator review
10. Strategy Router decision
11. PaperOps Gate Interface review
12. Existing guarded PaperOps lifecycle
13. Learning And Attribution Ledger
14. Recursive improvement proposal
15. Governance approval review
16. Self-healing repair routing if needed
17. Next-cycle instruction update
```

The loop should preserve the difference between:

- research evidence,
- shadow evidence,
- current paper lifecycle evidence,
- proof evidence,
- system health evidence,
- and governance approval.

These should never be collapsed into one generic "confidence" score.

### 17.4 Recursive Cycle States

Each cycle should end in one of these states.

`cycle_ready_for_research`:

```text
QSASE has enough current context to run source-price discovery and pattern
search.
```

`cycle_degraded_research_only`:

```text
QSASE can run partial research, but source coverage, model readiness, quantum
review, or historical memory is degraded. No promotion toward paper-review
routing is allowed from degraded-only evidence.
```

`cycle_strategy_proposal_ready`:

```text
Universal pattern search and labs produced a hypothesis that can enter Strategy
Foundry or Strategy Router review.
```

`cycle_shadow_only`:

```text
The hypothesis is interesting but not paper-review eligible. It should remain
in shadow replay or historical testing.
```

`cycle_paper_review_candidate`:

```text
The Strategy Router and upstream filters produced a candidate suitable for the
PaperOps Gate Interface.
```

`cycle_paperops_handoff_held`:

```text
The candidate was held by PaperOps Gate Interface or existing PaperOps checks.
The hold reason is preserved for learning.
```

`cycle_paper_lifecycle_observed`:

```text
A real guarded paper lifecycle event occurred and can be referenced for
learning and paper proof ledger review.
```

`cycle_learning_proposal_ready`:

```text
The Learning And Attribution Ledger produced a proposal for human or governance
review.
```

`cycle_repair_requested`:

```text
The blocker is a system defect, stale artifact, failed check, degraded route,
or missing input. It must be queued for repair instead of described as
strategy discipline.
```

`cycle_blocked_safety_boundary`:

```text
The cycle encountered a hard authority boundary such as live capital, broker
bypass, Telegram command authority, proof-credit bypass, or simulated elapsed
time. No improvement or trading action may proceed until the boundary is
resolved.
```

### 17.5 Inputs

The Recursive Improvement Contract should consume:

- QSASE Self-Model.
- Universal Source-Price Pattern Matrix.
- Historical Source-Price Memory.
- Full-Universe Pattern Search.
- Linear Pattern Recognition Lab.
- Nonlinear And Quantum Pattern Lab.
- Strategy Foundry outputs.
- Akber Filter outputs.
- Shadow Strategy Simulator outputs.
- Strategy Router outputs.
- PaperOps Gate Interface outputs.
- Existing PaperOps summary artifacts.
- Paper lifecycle artifacts.
- Paper proof ledger references.
- Learning And Attribution Ledger outputs.
- Source health records.
- Model readiness records.
- Quantum readiness records.
- Dashboard mirror state.
- Telegram delivery state.
- Self-healing repair records.
- Learning approval records.
- Existing Phase 6 proposal records.
- Existing edge memory ledger records.

It should never rely on a single summary string when structured evidence is
available.

### 17.6 Output Artifacts

QSASE should create:

```text
data/runtime/qsase_recursive_improvement_contract.json
data/runtime/qsase_recursive_improvement_contract.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_recursive_improvement_contract_history.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_recursive_improvement_contract_events.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_recursive_cycle_records.jsonl
data/runtime/qsase_improvement_proposals.json
data/runtime/qsase_self_healing_repair_queue.json
data/runtime/qsase_recursive_guardrail_audit.json
data/runtime/qsase_next_cycle_instruction_packet.json
```

The summary artifact should expose:

```text
status
generated_at
cycle_id
cycle_state
self_model_state
source_matrix_state
historical_memory_state
pattern_search_state
linear_lab_state
nonlinear_quantum_lab_state
strategy_foundry_state
akber_filter_state
shadow_simulator_state
strategy_router_state
paperops_gate_state
paperops_cycle_state
learning_ledger_state
improvement_proposal_count
repair_request_count
approval_required_count
approved_change_count
applied_change_count
safety_blocker_count
proof_credit_allowed
paper_order_created_count
broker_write_count
live_capital_enabled
```

`approved_change_count` and `applied_change_count` should be zero in the first
implementation unless an explicit future approval-and-apply module exists.

### 17.7 Recursive Cycle Record Contract

Each cycle record should use a schema like:

```json
{
  "cycle_id": "qsase-recursive-cycle-2026-06-28-001",
  "status": "cycle_learning_proposal_ready",
  "generated_at": "2026-06-28T12:00:00Z",
  "cycle_window": {
    "calendar_date": "2026-06-28",
    "uses_actual_calendar_dates": true,
    "backfilled": false,
    "simulated_elapsed_time": false
  },
  "input_states": {
    "self_model": "ready",
    "source_matrix": "ready",
    "historical_memory": "ready",
    "pattern_search": "ready",
    "linear_lab": "ready",
    "nonlinear_quantum_lab": "review_complete",
    "strategy_foundry": "hypothesis_ready",
    "akber_filter": "pass",
    "shadow_simulator": "candidate_supported",
    "strategy_router": "paper_review_candidate",
    "paperops_gate": "eligible_for_existing_paperops_review",
    "paperops_cycle": "ready_idle",
    "learning_ledger": "proposal_recorded_not_applied"
  },
  "cycle_decision": {
    "primary_result": "strategy_weight_proposal_created",
    "secondary_result": "keep_quantum_review_required",
    "why_not_trading_now": "existing PaperOps decides submission eligibility; QSASE created no order",
    "next_cycle_focus": "watch event-probability lag persistence and duplicate exposure state"
  },
  "proposal_summary": {
    "proposal_count": 2,
    "approval_required_count": 2,
    "applied_change_count": 0,
    "top_proposal": "increase energy_event_probability_lag strategy review weight by 0.03"
  },
  "repair_summary": {
    "repair_request_count": 0,
    "codex_reprompt_required": false,
    "top_repair_reason": null
  },
  "authority": {
    "strategy_mutation_allowed": false,
    "model_weight_mutation_allowed": false,
    "trust_score_mutation_allowed": false,
    "policy_mutation_allowed": false,
    "paper_order_allowed": false,
    "broker_write_allowed": false,
    "proof_credit_allowed": false,
    "live_capital_enabled": false
  }
}
```

The record should be compact enough for machine validation and explicit enough
for dashboard review.

### 17.8 Proposal-First Improvement

Every improvement must pass through a proposal state before it can become an
active change.

Proposal states:

`proposal_blocked_insufficient_evidence`:

```text
The idea is interesting but lacks enough real, shadow, historical, or
cross-source evidence.
```

`proposal_ready_pending_review`:

```text
The evidence is strong enough for review, but no change has been applied.
```

`proposal_rejected_by_governance`:

```text
The proposal was reviewed and rejected.
```

`proposal_approved_pending_apply_module`:

```text
The proposal was approved, but a separate apply module must execute and audit
the change.
```

`proposal_applied_by_approved_module`:

```text
A separate explicit module applied the change and wrote an audit record.
```

QSASE itself should only produce the first two states in the initial
implementation.

### 17.9 Improvement Proposal Types

QSASE should support these proposal types:

- `strategy_family_weight_review`
- `strategy_family_split_review`
- `strategy_family_merge_review`
- `strategy_family_retirement_review`
- `new_strategy_family_review`
- `akber_filter_threshold_review`
- `source_trust_review`
- `source_coverage_repair_review`
- `model_weight_review`
- `quantum_review_priority_review`
- `router_policy_review`
- `paperops_gate_precheck_review`
- `risk_budget_review`
- `execution_policy_review`
- `dashboard_explanation_review`
- `telegram_message_quality_review`
- `self_healing_repair_review`

Each proposal should state:

```text
what should change
why it should change
which evidence supports it
which evidence contradicts it
which evidence class is strongest
whether real paper evidence exists
whether shadow evidence exists
whether historical evidence exists
whether quantum review was useful
whether Akber's filter agrees
whether PaperOps route state affected the outcome
what risk the change introduces
who or what must approve it
whether anything was applied
```

The final field should almost always be:

```text
applied = false
```

### 17.10 Self-Healing Branch

Recursive improvement must include self-healing, but self-healing must be
scoped narrowly.

Self-healing should trigger when QSASE detects:

- Missing runtime artifact.
- Invalid schema.
- Stale cockpit mirror.
- Stale paper account mirror.
- Missing idempotency ledger.
- Missing source matrix.
- Missing historical memory.
- Failed source adapter.
- Broken provider credential state.
- Telegram delivery failure.
- Dashboard export failure.
- Failed PaperOps check.
- Degraded route state.
- Contradictory live-capital flag.
- Missing Q-CTRL state.
- Broken quantum review artifact.
- Broken learning artifact.
- Failed validation script.

Self-healing should create a repair queue record, not hide the defect.

The repair queue should include:

```text
repair_id
repair_state
trigger_reason
failed_artifact
failed_check
detected_at
last_good_artifact_ref
recommended_fix_scope
forbidden_actions
codex_reprompt_required
repair_prompt
post_repair_validation_command
post_repair_source_of_truth
```

For PaperOps repair, the post-repair validation command should remain:

```text
.venv/bin/python scripts/run_paperops_autonomous_pass.py
```

and the post-repair source of truth should remain:

```text
data/runtime/paperops_autonomous_pass_summary.json
```

QSASE must not invent alternate validation commands for the canonical PaperOps
route.

### 17.11 Self-RePrompt Contract

The user requirement for a self-healing automated system means the automation
should not stop at:

```text
status = degraded
```

It should emit:

```text
status = repair_requested
codex_reprompt_required = true
repair_prompt = narrow repair prompt
forbidden_actions = explicit forbidden list
validation_command = exact canonical command
source_of_truth = exact canonical artifact
```

The repair prompt should instruct the next automation or Codex pass to:

- read the failed summary first,
- use failed commands, blockers, validation errors, parsed output, stdout tail,
  and stderr tail as evidence,
- apply the narrowest repo or runtime-artifact repair,
- preserve the real paper calendar,
- avoid forced trades,
- avoid secret edits,
- avoid live credentials,
- avoid live capital,
- avoid live broker endpoints,
- avoid Q-CTRL bypass,
- avoid proof-credit grants,
- avoid Telegram command enablement,
- rerun the exact validation command,
- report from the canonical artifact.

The self-reprompt contract should never include trading instructions. It is a
repair loop, not a trading loop.

### 17.12 Valid No-Action Versus Repair

QSASE must distinguish valid no-action from repair-worthy failure.

Valid no-action examples:

- No source-price pattern passed quorum.
- Akber's filter held weak confirmation.
- Strategy Router selected shadow-only.
- PaperOps Gate detected duplicate exposure.
- Daily drawdown gate blocked.
- Market was closed.
- No fresh eligible setup existed.
- Existing open orders were pending fill.
- Q-CTRL paper consultation hold remained active.

Repair-worthy examples:

- Source matrix artifact missing.
- Paper account mirror stale beyond policy.
- PaperOps command failed.
- Validation errors occurred.
- Required runtime summary was not refreshed.
- Dashboard mirror contradicted runtime artifact.
- Telegram delivery failed after content gates passed.
- Idempotency ledger was missing.
- Live-capital flag contradicted paper-only state.

The dashboard should not call repair-worthy failures "discipline." It should
call them repair requests.

### 17.13 Calendar Boundary

Recursive improvement must preserve actual calendar time.

Rules:

- No backfilled cycle can count as a current paper-cycle improvement.
- No simulated elapsed time can advance the 30-day paper growth trial.
- No historical backtest can create current proof evidence.
- No shadow replay can become a paper proof ledger entry.
- No recursive cycle can grant paper proof ledger credit.
- No rerun can pretend a missed market day occurred.
- Same-day repeated runs should dedupe cycle identity unless a materially new
  event or artifact appears.

The recursive system can learn from historical data. It cannot rewrite the
paper-trial calendar.

### 17.14 Evidence Promotion Rules

QSASE should promote evidence slowly.

Suggested promotion ladder:

```text
raw source observation
-> normalized source-price record
-> candidate pattern
-> repeated historical pattern
-> linear or nonlinear lab result
-> quantum-reviewed research pattern
-> Strategy Foundry hypothesis
-> shadow strategy
-> router-reviewed paper candidate
-> PaperOps-reviewed setup
-> real paper lifecycle evidence
-> attribution record
-> improvement proposal
-> governance-approved change
-> separately audited application
```

No step should skip directly from raw observation to active strategy mutation.
No step should skip directly from backtest result to paper proof.

### 17.15 Overfit And Regime Drift Controls

Recursive systems can overfit themselves if every cycle chases the last
outcome. QSASE must explicitly guard against this.

Controls:

- Require out-of-sample evidence for historical patterns.
- Separate in-sample and out-of-sample backtest windows.
- Track whether the pattern survives across regimes.
- Track whether evidence depends on one source.
- Track whether evidence depends on one instrument.
- Track whether one winning paper trade is being over-weighted.
- Track whether model explanations changed after seeing the result.
- Track whether quantum review added information or only restated uncertainty.
- Track whether Akber's filter is becoming too strict or too loose.
- Track whether router decisions are clustering into one state.
- Track whether PaperOps holds are safety-valid or route-defect-driven.

The recursive contract should prefer:

```text
small proposal, high auditability
```

over:

```text
large change, weak explanation
```

### 17.16 Exploration And Exploitation

QSASE should balance exploration and exploitation.

Exploration means:

- testing new cross-source relationships,
- searching weaker but plausible nonlinear patterns,
- creating new strategy families,
- running shadow strategies,
- expanding source coverage,
- checking unusual market expressions.

Exploitation means:

- focusing on strategy families with evidence,
- routing cleaner candidates faster,
- maintaining strict filter discipline,
- reducing weak-source noise,
- reusing proven lineage structures,
- improving paper-review readiness.

The recursive contract should not let exploitation become tunnel vision or let
exploration become random strategy churn. Both should be governed by evidence
classes and proposal review.

### 17.17 Component-Level Recursion

QSASE should improve each component differently.

Source Universe:

```text
Improve coverage, freshness, quality, quorum, and source trust proposals.
```

Historical Memory:

```text
Improve retention, replayability, deduplication, and source-price alignment.
```

Linear Lab:

```text
Improve transparent factor tests, lag tests, event studies, and simple
baselines.
```

Nonlinear And Quantum Lab:

```text
Improve pattern search over nonlinear interactions, ambiguity, path dependence,
and quantum-review usefulness.
```

Local Gemma:

```text
Improve triage, compression, categorization, and contradiction detection.
```

Frontier Gemini:

```text
Improve critique quality, hypothesis challenge, source-quorum discipline, and
failure-mode discovery.
```

Akber Filter:

```text
Improve selectivity, market confirmation, veto reasons, and stage-gate clarity.
```

Strategy Router:

```text
Improve state selection, no-trade clarity, and paper-review candidate quality.
```

PaperOps:

```text
Improve handoff completeness, idempotency, route readiness, lifecycle polling,
and postmortem evidence.
```

Dashboard And Telegram:

```text
Improve concise decision records, avoid repetition, and expose blockers without
turning status into commands.
```

Self-Healing:

```text
Improve detection, repair prompts, validation commands, and post-repair
evidence capture.
```

### 17.18 Governance Review

The recursive loop should end with governance review whenever it proposes a
change that could affect strategy behavior.

Governance review should decide:

- whether evidence is sufficient,
- whether risk is acceptable,
- whether the proposal is overfit,
- whether the proposal conflicts with existing safety rules,
- whether the proposal requires more shadow testing,
- whether the proposal requires more paper evidence,
- whether the proposal can be applied,
- which separate module may apply it,
- how the application will be audited,
- how rollback would work.

Until governance approves and an apply module records the change, QSASE should
display:

```text
proposal only, not applied
```

### 17.19 Relationship To PaperOps

Recursive improvement must not replace PaperOps.

PaperOps remains the only route for:

- guarded paper setup review,
- risk checks,
- execution checks,
- idempotency enforcement,
- Q-CTRL paper consultation holds,
- guarded Alpaca Paper submission,
- paper lifecycle polling,
- paper proof ledger review.

QSASE can learn from PaperOps. It can propose improvements to upstream
strategy routing and handoff quality. It cannot bypass PaperOps because a
recursive cycle is confident.

### 17.20 Relationship To Daily Learning

QSASE should interoperate with the existing daily-learning loop.

The daily-learning loop can provide:

- paper lifecycle observations,
- postmortem packets,
- learning briefs,
- Telegram human briefs,
- model-weight proposal context,
- source-trust proposal context,
- architect learning recommendations.

QSASE should not duplicate those modules blindly. It should consume their
records and extend them with full-universe pattern context, component
attribution, and recursive improvement proposals.

If daily learning fails only at notification delivery, QSASE should classify
that as visibility degradation, not strategy failure.

### 17.21 Dashboard Representation

The dashboard should show recursive improvement as a compact decision trail.

Recommended module:

```text
Recursive Improvement
Cycle: 2026-06-28-001
State: proposal ready
Learned from: no-trade + shadow replay + source-price lag
Proposal: hold Akber threshold, keep strategy in shadow
Applied: no
Next: watch for market confirmation
```

For repair:

```text
Recursive Improvement
Cycle: 2026-06-28-002
State: repair requested
Issue: paper mirror stale
Scope: repo/runtime artifact repair only
Validation: run canonical PaperOps pass
Applied: no strategy change
```

The dashboard should avoid vague statements like:

```text
Qadam has improved its strategy intelligence.
```

It should say what changed, what evidence supports it, and whether anything
was applied.

### 17.22 Telegram Representation

Telegram should receive only short review notes.

Allowed learning note:

```text
Qadam learning note
Cycle: proposal only
Finding: Akber filter blocked weak setup correctly
Proposal: keep threshold unchanged
Applied: no
```

Allowed repair note:

```text
Qadam repair note
Issue: paper mirror stale
Action: repair queued
Validation: PaperOps pass required
No trade submitted
```

Telegram must not receive:

- strategy mutation approvals,
- broker instructions,
- live-capital language,
- command syntax,
- long generic upgrade summaries,
- repeated boilerplate,
- fake certainty.

### 17.23 Authority Boundary

The Recursive Improvement Contract must never:

- Create Telegram commands.
- Create trade candidates directly.
- Create qualified setups directly.
- Create risk approvals.
- Create execution approvals.
- Stage paper orders.
- Submit paper orders.
- Write broker records.
- Call broker endpoints.
- Call live endpoints.
- Enable live capital.
- Load live credentials.
- Edit secrets.
- Bypass Q-CTRL.
- Bypass PaperOps.
- Bypass Signal Integrity.
- Bypass Strategy Lead review.
- Bypass Risk Agent sizing.
- Bypass Execution Policy.
- Bypass idempotency.
- Bypass duplicate exposure checks.
- Bypass daily drawdown checks.
- Apply model weights.
- Apply source-trust scores.
- Apply strategy weights.
- Apply policy changes.
- Grant paper proof ledger credit.
- Simulate elapsed time in the 30-day paper growth trial.

It may:

- Record cycle state.
- Record recursive observations.
- Record proposals.
- Record repair requests.
- Record next-cycle instructions.
- Feed dashboard decision records.
- Feed short Telegram review notes.
- Queue governance review.
- Queue self-healing repair.

### 17.24 Build Contract

Implementation should add:

```text
orchestrator/qsase_recursive_improvement_contract.py
scripts/check_qsase_recursive_improvement_contract.py
```

The module should expose:

```text
build_recursive_improvement_contract(settings)
load_recursive_improvement_contract(settings)
validate_recursive_improvement_contract(payload)
write_recursive_improvement_contract(payload, settings)
build_recursive_cycle_record(context)
classify_recursive_cycle_state(context)
build_improvement_proposals(context)
build_self_healing_repair_queue(context)
build_next_cycle_instruction_packet(context)
validate_recursive_authority_boundary(payload)
```

The check script should validate:

- Required input states are present or missing inputs are explicit.
- Cycle id is stable and deduped for same-day repeated runs.
- Actual calendar dates are used.
- Backfill flag is false.
- Simulated elapsed time is false.
- Evidence classes remain separated.
- Research, shadow, paper, and proof evidence are not collapsed.
- Proposal records are present when improvement is claimed.
- Proposal records are not applied.
- Repair-worthy failures create repair requests.
- Valid no-action states are not mislabeled as failures.
- System defects are not mislabeled as strategy discipline.
- PaperOps canonical validation command is preserved when PaperOps repair is
  requested.
- Strategy mutation is false.
- Model-weight mutation is false.
- Trust-score mutation is false.
- Policy mutation is false.
- Paper order creation is zero.
- Broker write count is zero.
- Proof credit is false.
- Live capital is false.
- Dashboard summary uses decision records.
- Telegram summary is short, specific, and command-disabled.

The first implementation should be conservative. It should prove the recursive
contract can record, classify, propose, and request repairs before any
downstream module is allowed to apply a change.

### 17.25 Recursive Improvement Contract Summary

The Recursive Improvement Contract is the spine that makes QSASE an evolution
of Qadam rather than a separate feature.

It turns the whole architecture into a closed learning loop: self-model,
source universe, historical memory, full-universe search, linear testing,
nonlinear and quantum review, strategy formation, Akber filtering, shadow
simulation, routing, PaperOps review, paper lifecycle evidence, attribution,
proposal, governance, repair, and next-cycle focus.

The contract's discipline is what matters. QSASE can keep improving its
understanding of its own stack and its trading universe, but it cannot use
that understanding to bypass safety. It learns, proposes, repairs, and
explains. It does not silently mutate strategy, create orders, touch live
capital, or grant proof credit.

## 18. Dashboard Representation

The Dashboard Representation defines how QSASE becomes visible to humans
without turning the dashboard into a wall of generated prose.

It asks:

```text
How does the dashboard show what QSASE is thinking, testing, rejecting,
holding, proposing, repairing, and learning without implying unauthorized
trading authority?
```

The dashboard should be a readable operating map. It should help the Fund
Manager answer:

```text
Is the paper portfolio going up or down?
What does the portfolio currently contain?
What trades has Qadam already made?
What is Qadam seeing?
Which strategies does it currently trust?
Why does it trust them?
Which patterns is it detecting?
Which trades is it thinking about making?
What is blocked?
What is being tested only in shadow?
What is eligible for PaperOps review?
What has actually happened in the paper account?
What did Qadam learn?
What changed, if anything?
What is the next allowed action?
```

It should not be a generic AI explanation layer. It should be a public-safe,
read-only mirror of structured backend decision records.

### 18.1 Design Principle

The dashboard principle is:

```text
decision records over essays
```

Every visible QSASE claim should be reducible to:

```text
State
Reason
Evidence
Blocker
Next allowed action
Authority boundary
```

The dashboard should not say:

```text
Qadam has improved its trading intelligence using advanced AI and quantum
insights.
```

It should say:

```text
QSASE state: proposal only
Trusted strategy: energy event probability lag
Reason: ACLED, Kalshi, and XLE moved in expected sequence
Blocker: duplicate exposure check pending
Next: wait for PaperOps gate
Authority: no order submitted
```

This is how QSASE becomes legible without becoming slop.

### 18.2 Dashboard Role

The dashboard is read-only.

It may:

- Show the paper portfolio value line graph.
- Show current holdings and cash.
- Show exposure, drawdown, and realized / unrealized paper P&L.
- Show trading history and paper lifecycle state.
- Show current QSASE state.
- Show strategy universe state.
- Show strategy families overall and strategies currently in play.
- Show source universe coverage.
- Show connected data source categories and individual data sources.
- Show trading universe coverage.
- Show source-price pattern records.
- Show linear and nonlinear pattern lab results.
- Show current trade intents and candidate setups under review.
- Show strategy hypotheses.
- Show Akber Filter state.
- Show shadow simulator state.
- Show Strategy Router decisions.
- Show PaperOps Gate Interface handoffs, holds, and rejections.
- Show why-not-trading-now reasons.
- Show learning attribution.
- Show recursive improvement proposals.
- Show self-healing repair requests.
- Show paper proof ledger references.
- Show 30-day paper growth trial state.

It must not:

- Place trades.
- Approve trades.
- Create Telegram commands.
- Create qualified setups.
- Submit paper orders.
- Write brokers.
- Call live endpoints.
- Enable live capital.
- Grant paper proof ledger credit.
- Hide degraded states behind positive language.
- Display secrets.
- Display raw credentials.
- Use stale artifacts without saying they are stale.

The dashboard is an operating mirror, not an operating authority.

### 18.3 Placement In Existing Dashboard Flow

QSASE should fit into the existing user journey rather than create a separate
parallel dashboard.

Recommended high-level flow:

```text
Overview
-> Portfolio Value & Return
-> Current Portfolio
-> Trading History
-> Source Intelligence Network
-> Trading Strategy Universe
-> Pattern & Opportunity Lab
-> Decision Room: Current Decision, then Decisions Brewing (trade intents)
-> Mission Snapshot

Evidence
-> Data source categories and connected sources
-> Trading universe and paperable instruments
-> Universal Source-Price Pattern Matrix
-> Historical Source-Price Memory

Reasoning
-> Self-Model / Investment Team
-> Full-Universe Pattern Search
-> Linear Pattern Lab
-> Nonlinear And Quantum Pattern Lab
-> Strategy Foundry
-> Akber Filter
-> Strategy Router

Trades
-> Portfolio value time series
-> Current holdings
-> PaperOps Gate Interface
-> Paper lifecycle
-> Open orders
-> Open positions
-> Closed paper trades
-> Trading history
-> Paper proof ledger references

Operations
-> Phase 0 PaperOps reliability baseline
-> Recursive Improvement Contract
-> Learning And Attribution Ledger
-> Self-healing repair queue
-> Communications state
-> Governance state
-> Technical diagnostics
```

Overview should be commercially grounded: portfolio value first, then holdings,
history, sources, strategies, patterns, current trade intents, and final gate
state. Detailed raw ledgers should still live in dedicated views.

### 18.4 Overview Contract

The Overview should answer the minimum operating questions.

Recommended QSASE Overview fields:

- Paper portfolio value time series.
- Current paper portfolio value.
- Cash.
- Exposure.
- Drawdown.
- Open P&L.
- Closed P&L.
- Current holdings.
- Latest trade history records.
- QSASE state.
- Trusted strategy today.
- Why trusted.
- Why not trading now.
- Current trade intents.
- Current linear and nonlinear pattern candidates.
- Fresh eligible paper-review candidates.
- PaperOps gate state.
- Open order count.
- Open position count.
- Closed paper trade count.
- Learning state.
- Repair state.
- Quantum review state.
- Live-capital boundary.

Recommended display:

```text
Portfolio & QSASE Snapshot
Portfolio: GBP 101,240, +1.24% paper return
Holdings: XLE, GLD, NVDA
Latest trade: XLE long, open, +0.8% unrealized
Top strategy: energy event probability lag
Top pattern: ACLED + Kalshi lead XLE by 3-7 days
Trade intent: XLE add-on under review
Why not trading now: duplicate exposure check pending
Authority: paper-only, dashboard read-only, no order submitted by QSASE
```

This replaces several repetitive dashboard blocks with one operating summary.

### 18.5 Portfolio Value And Mission Snapshot Integration

The dashboard should keep the paper portfolio value line graph at the top of
the main narrative. The line graph is the fastest way for a non-technical Fund
Manager to see whether Qadam's paper operation is creating or losing value.

The portfolio graph should show:

- paper portfolio value over time,
- daily and cumulative paper return,
- cash,
- exposure,
- drawdown,
- open P&L,
- closed P&L,
- trade markers,
- strategy labels on trade markers where safe,
- paper proof ledger marker for eligible closed trades.

Mission Snapshot should sit beside or below the graph as a compact state strip.
It should not displace the portfolio graph.

Mission Snapshot should include:

- Current operating mode.
- Paper-only authority state.
- Latest QSASE cycle state.
- Current paper-trial day.
- Current proof boundary.
- Latest blocker.
- Next allowed action.
- Current dashboard freshness state.

Example:

```text
Mission Snapshot
Mode: paper-only research and guarded paper operations
QSASE: recursive cycle ready, proposal only
Paper trial: actual calendar only
Blocker: no fresh eligible PaperOps submit
Next: wait for qualified setup or repair request
Authority: dashboard cannot place orders
```

Mission Snapshot should not repeat holdings, trading history, source rows,
strategy rows, pattern rows, or learning details. It should tell the user what
state Qadam is in and where to look next.

### 18.6 Strategy Universe Integration

The Strategy Universe module should be the main home for QSASE strategy
explanations.

It should show:

- Active strategy families.
- All strategy families known to Qadam.
- Strategies currently in play.
- Strategy hypotheses under review.
- Shadow-only strategies.
- Paper-review candidates.
- Retired or rejected hypotheses.
- Akber Filter state per strategy.
- Quantum review state per strategy.
- Latest source-price pattern behind each strategy.
- Evidence class.
- Paperability state.
- Current router state.
- Latest learning proposal.

Recommended row format:

```text
Energy event probability lag
State: paper-review candidate
Evidence: ACLED + Kalshi + XLE
Akber: pass
Quantum: reviewed for nonlinear lag
Router: PaperOps gate candidate
Blocker: duplicate exposure check
Applied change: none
```

Strategy Universe should not show long prose paragraphs. If a strategy cannot
be explained in a concise row, the underlying artifact is not ready for
dashboard display.

### 18.7 Current Portfolio And Trading History Integration

The Current Portfolio and Trading History modules should keep QSASE connected
to reality.

It should show:

- Portfolio value line graph.
- Trade markers on the portfolio value graph.
- Current holdings.
- Current cash.
- Current exposure.
- Open P&L.
- Closed P&L.
- Realized and unrealized paper P&L.
- Fresh eligible paper-review candidate count.
- Duplicate submit count with idempotency wording.
- Submitted paper order count.
- Open order count.
- Open position count.
- Closed paper trade count.
- Paper proof ledger state.
- 30-day paper growth trial state.
- Current paper account mirror freshness.
- Guarded Alpaca Paper route state.
- Full trading history table with submitted, filled, held, rejected, canceled,
  closed, and postmortem states.

QSASE additions:

- Which strategy family created the latest paper-review candidate.
- Which Research Goal lineage produced it.
- Whether QSASE created only a handoff, not an order.
- Whether PaperOps accepted, held, rejected, or submitted through its own route.
- Whether learning attribution exists for closed paper outcomes.
- Which pattern and data source packet supported each trade.
- Which invalidation condition or lifecycle rule currently matters.

Example:

```text
Current Portfolio
Value: GBP 101,240
Cash: GBP 42,800
Exposure: 58%
Holdings: XLE +0.8%, GLD -0.2%, NVDA +1.1%
Latest trade: XLE long, PaperOps submitted, lifecycle open
Proof: paper proof ledger only, no simulated credit
```

This avoids a false split between "strategy intelligence" and actual paper
account state.

### 18.8 Control Plane Integration

The Control Plane should show QSASE as one lane in the operating map.

Recommended lanes:

- Source intake.
- Source-price matrix.
- Pattern labs.
- Strategy Foundry.
- Akber Filter.
- Strategy Router.
- PaperOps Gate Interface.
- Existing PaperOps lifecycle.
- Learning And Attribution Ledger.
- Recursive Improvement Contract.
- Self-healing repair queue.
- Dashboard and Telegram visibility.

Each lane should show:

```text
status
latest artifact
latest check
blocker
next allowed action
authority boundary
```

The Control Plane should make failure location obvious. A stale paper mirror
should appear as a route-state defect, not as a strategy failure. A missing
quantum artifact should appear as an advanced-review hold, not as a generic
warning.

### 18.9 Source And Trading Universe View

QSASE needs a dedicated view that shows the full data universe and full trading
universe together.

It should answer:

```text
Which data source categories are connected?
Which individual data sources are connected?
Which sources are available?
Which sources are stale, degraded, missing, or credential-gated?
Which instruments can currently be evaluated?
Which instruments are only research expressions?
Which instruments are paperable through existing guarded routes?
Which source families map to which trading instruments?
Where is source quorum strong?
Where is source quorum weak?
```

Recommended matrix columns:

- Source category.
- Source family.
- Source name.
- Source state.
- Coverage window.
- Freshness.
- Trust posture.
- Quorum contribution.
- Related instruments.
- Pattern count.
- Strategy family count.
- Paperability.
- Latest blocker.

Example:

```text
Category: prediction markets
Source: Kalshi
State: configured or credential-gated
Maps to: event probability strategies
Related instruments: sector ETFs, index ETFs, macro proxies
Paperability: indirect only through Alpaca paper instruments
Authority: source input only
```

This makes clear that plugging in a source does not mean Qadam can trade on
that source directly.

### 18.10 Universal Pattern Lab View

The Universal Pattern Lab view should merge:

- Universal Source-Price Pattern Matrix.
- Historical Source-Price Memory.
- Full-Universe Pattern Search.
- Linear Pattern Recognition Lab.
- Nonlinear And Quantum Pattern Lab.

The view should not show five disconnected panels. It should show the pattern
pipeline.

Recommended pipeline:

```text
Source-price matrix
-> historical memory
-> full-universe search
-> linear lab
-> nonlinear and quantum lab
-> strategy hypothesis
```

Recommended row:

```text
Pattern: event odds lead sector ETF by 3-7 days
Sources: Kalshi + ACLED + Yahoo Finance
Linear result: positive lag correlation
Nonlinear result: regime-dependent
Quantum review: useful ambiguity check
Strategy mapping: energy event probability lag
Trade being considered: XLE long add-on
State: shadow supported, PaperOps review pending
```

This keeps the lab visible without drowning the user in raw backtest tables.

### 18.11 Self-Model View

The Self-Model view should explain how Qadam understands its own stack.

It should show:

- Python orchestrator role.
- Local Gemma role.
- Frontier Gemini role.
- IBM quantum gates oracle role.
- Source adapters role.
- Historical memory role.
- PaperOps role.
- Dashboard role.
- Telegram role.
- Known limitations.
- Current degraded components.
- Current authority boundaries.

Recommended language:

```text
Local Gemma: triage and compression
Frontier Gemini: critique and hypothesis challenge
Quantum: nonlinear ambiguity review, not trade authority
Python orchestrator: deterministic gates and artifact writes
PaperOps: only guarded paper submit route
```

The Self-Model should be operational, not philosophical. It should explain how
the architecture changes strategy behavior today.

### 18.12 Decision Room

Current trade intents, Strategy Router state, and why-not-trading-now state
should be visible together on one page. The current Router/PaperOps outcome
comes first; Decisions Brewing follows with the supporting intent records.

The Decisions Brewing section should show what Qadam is thinking of making without
calling those ideas trades.

Recommended trade intent fields:

- proposed instrument,
- proposed direction,
- strategy family,
- source packet,
- linear pattern evidence,
- nonlinear or quantum review evidence,
- Akber Filter state,
- invalidation condition,
- risk state,
- idempotency state,
- duplicate exposure state,
- PaperOps eligibility state,
- blocker,
- next allowed action.

Recommended display:

```text
Current Decision
Outcome: hold
Reason: no fresh eligible submit because duplicate exposure is pending
Based on: 4 trade-intent reviews
Next: wait for a fresh distinct setup

Decisions Brewing
Trade Intent
Instrument: XLE
Direction: long add-on
Strategy: energy event probability lag
Pattern: ACLED + Kalshi lead XLE by 3-7 days
Router state: hold_duplicate_exposure
Reason: existing XLE exposure already expresses the setup
Next: wait for a distinct source packet or invalidation window
Authority: no order submitted
```

Allowed router states:

- `paper_review_candidate`
- `shadow_only`
- `watchlist_only`
- `reject`
- `hold_missing_evidence`
- `hold_route_unavailable`
- `repair_requested`
- `blocked_safety_boundary`

Why-not-trading-now should be specific:

```text
No fresh eligible submit because duplicate exposure is pending.
```

not:

```text
Qadam is waiting for optimal conditions.
```

### 18.13 Learning Ledger View

The Learning Ledger view should show what QSASE learned and whether anything
changed.

Recommended display:

```text
Learning Ledger
Latest evidence: no-trade decision
Attribution: Akber filter helped
Later check: market did not confirm
Proposal: keep filter strict
Applied: no
```

Fields:

- Latest attribution state.
- Latest evidence class.
- Credited component.
- Blamed component.
- Real paper evidence count.
- Shadow evidence count.
- Backtest evidence count.
- Proposal count.
- Approval-required count.
- Applied update count.
- Proof boundary.

The view must never imply that learning automatically changed strategy.

### 18.14 Recursive Improvement View

The Recursive Improvement view should show the loop state.

Recommended display:

```text
Recursive Improvement
Cycle: 2026-06-28-001
State: proposal ready
Learned from: shadow replay + non-trade + source-price memory
Proposal: keep strategy in shadow
Applied: no
Next: wait for fresh cross-source confirmation
```

For repair:

```text
Recursive Improvement
Cycle: repair requested
Issue: cockpit mirror stale
Scope: dashboard/runtime artifact repair only
Validation: rerun status export and dashboard checks
Applied strategy change: no
```

This view should be in Operations, not Overview.

### 18.15 Decision Record Schema

Every dashboard QSASE card should use the same decision-record shape.

```json
{
  "decision_record_id": "qsase-dashboard-router-2026-06-28-001",
  "module": "strategy_router",
  "state": "hold_route_unavailable",
  "headline": "Paper-review candidate held by stale paper mirror.",
  "strategy_family": "energy_event_probability_lag",
  "evidence": [
    "ACLED event escalation",
    "Kalshi odds movement",
    "XLE confirmation pending"
  ],
  "blocker": "paper_account_mirror_stale",
  "next_allowed_action": "refresh mirror and rerun guarded PaperOps checks",
  "applied_change": false,
  "paper_order_created": false,
  "proof_credit_allowed": false,
  "live_capital_enabled": false,
  "artifact_refs": [
    "data/runtime/qsase_strategy_router_decisions.json",
    "data/runtime/qsase_paperops_gate_interface.json"
  ]
}
```

The dashboard should render this record, not generate an essay from it.

### 18.16 Status Language

QSASE status language should be short and consistent.

Allowed status labels:

- `Ready`
- `Watching`
- `Research only`
- `Shadow only`
- `Paper-review candidate`
- `Held`
- `Rejected`
- `Repair requested`
- `Blocked`
- `Stale`
- `Degraded`
- `Proposal only`
- `Applied by approved module`

Avoid:

- `online`
- `powered by AI`
- `smart`
- `autonomous trading active`
- `optimized`
- `high confidence` without evidence
- `learning complete`
- `quantum confirmed`
- `live-ready`
- `trade-ready` when no fresh eligible submit exists

If the state is mixed, prefer the more cautious label.

### 18.17 Anti-Repetition Rules

The dashboard should enforce anti-repetition rules.

Rules:

- One global authority summary only.
- One current why-not-trading-now reason only.
- One paper account state summary only.
- One source universe summary only.
- One strategy universe summary only.
- One router decision summary only.
- One repair queue summary only.
- Detailed ledgers live outside Overview.
- Repeated phrases are replaced by artifact links or counts.
- Generated prose is capped and must be source-backed.
- If two cards answer the same user question, merge them.

Examples of duplicate pairs to avoid:

- Mission brief plus system status plus safety copy.
- Strategy narrative plus trading strategies.
- Paper portfolio plus trade considerations.
- Operating flow plus mini-map plus oversight.
- Learning note plus recursive improvement note saying the same thing.
- Telegram state repeated in both Overview and Operations without a different
  purpose.

The dashboard should have one answer per question.

### 18.18 Anti-Slop Checks

QSASE dashboard checks should reject vague or repetitive output.

Checks should fail when:

- A QSASE card has no artifact reference.
- A QSASE card has no evidence field.
- A QSASE card has no blocker or explicit no-blocker state.
- A QSASE card has no next allowed action.
- A QSASE card omits authority boundary fields.
- A strategy explanation exceeds the allowed length.
- A dashboard card repeats another card's headline.
- A dashboard card says "AI", "advanced", "optimized", or "intelligent" without
  concrete evidence.
- A card says "learned" without an attribution record.
- A card says "trade-ready" without PaperOps route evidence.
- A card says "quantum" without quantum review state.
- A card says "paper proof" without paper proof ledger reference.
- A repair-worthy failure is described as strategy discipline.
- A stale artifact is presented as current.
- Live-capital state is missing.

Anti-slop checks should be automated, not subjective.

### 18.19 Public-Safe Rules

QSASE dashboard output must be public-safe even behind auth.

Do not display:

- secrets,
- raw credentials,
- environment variable values,
- broker account identifiers beyond safe labels,
- private token names paired with live values,
- raw Telegram identifiers if not needed,
- prompt text that could become operational authority,
- broker submit payloads,
- live endpoint URLs,
- unredacted provider errors containing secrets.

Allowed public-safe states:

```text
configured
missing
credential-gated
degraded
disabled
read-only
paper-only
live-capital off
```

### 18.20 Freshness And Staleness

Every QSASE dashboard module should expose freshness.

Fields:

- `generated_at`
- `artifact_mtime`
- `freshness_state`
- `stale_after_seconds`
- `source_artifact`
- `signature_state`
- `last_successful_check`
- `last_failed_check`

If dashboard state is stale, the UI should say:

```text
Dashboard mirror stale. Runtime artifact must be refreshed before this can be
used as current state.
```

It should not show the stale state as if it is current.

### 18.21 Dashboard Artifacts

QSASE should create a dashboard-ready artifact layer.

```text
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_status.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_decision_records.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_system_map.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_portfolio_value_series.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_current_portfolio.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_trading_history.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_source_network.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_strategy_universe.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_pattern_lab.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_trade_intents.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_learning_ledger.json
data/runtime/qsase_dashboard_repair_queue.json
```

The dashboard renderer should consume these compact view-model artifacts rather
than reinterpreting raw research ledgers in the browser.

### 18.22 View-Model Contract

The dashboard view model should include:

```text
schema_version
generated_at
public_safe
freshness_state
qsase_snapshot
mission_snapshot_fields
portfolio_value_series
portfolio_return_summary
current_portfolio_rows
trading_history_rows
strategy_universe_rows
strategies_currently_in_play
source_category_rows
connected_source_rows
source_trading_universe_rows
pattern_lab_rows
linear_pattern_rows
nonlinear_pattern_rows
trade_intent_rows
self_model_rows
router_decision_record
why_not_trading_now_record
paperops_gate_record
paper_account_trade_state
learning_ledger_rows
recursive_improvement_record
repair_queue_rows
authority_boundary
artifact_refs
```

The renderer should be dumb. It should display structured facts, not invent
interpretations.

### 18.23 Visual Hierarchy

Recommended visual hierarchy:

```text
1. Portfolio value and return
2. Current holdings and exposure
3. Trading history and lifecycle state
4. Connected data source categories and sources
5. Strategy universe and strategies currently in play
6. Linear and nonlinear pattern opportunities
7. Current trade intents
8. Router decision and why-not-trading-now
9. Learning/proposal state
10. Authority boundary
```

The dashboard should use the line graph, compact rows, badges, and connection
lines rather than long cards. It should show how paper portfolio reality leads
back to sources, strategies, patterns, trade intents, router state, PaperOps
state, and learning.

### 18.24 Example Dashboard Snapshot

Example:

```text
QSASE Snapshot
Portfolio: GBP 101,240, +1.24% paper return
Holdings: XLE, GLD, NVDA
Trading history: 78 closed paper trades
State: shadow-only
Trusted strategy: none for new paper review
Reason: best pattern lacks fresh market confirmation
Top pattern: event odds lead sector ETF
Akber: hold
Quantum: nonlinear review useful, not authority
Trade intent: none eligible
Router: shadow_only
PaperOps: no handoff
Learning: proposal only, not applied
Repair: none
Authority: paper-only, read-only dashboard
```

This is specific, concise, and honest.

### 18.25 Build Contract

Implementation should add:

```text
orchestrator/qsase_dashboard_view_model.py
scripts/check_qsase_dashboard_view_model.py
scripts/check_qsase_dashboard_anti_slop.py
```

The module should expose:

```text
build_qsase_dashboard_view_model(settings)
load_qsase_dashboard_view_model(settings)
validate_qsase_dashboard_view_model(payload)
write_qsase_dashboard_view_model(payload, settings)
build_qsase_decision_records(context)
build_qsase_system_map(context)
build_qsase_portfolio_value_series(context)
build_qsase_current_portfolio_rows(context)
build_qsase_trading_history_rows(context)
build_qsase_source_network_rows(context)
build_qsase_strategy_universe_rows(context)
build_qsase_pattern_lab_rows(context)
build_qsase_trade_intent_rows(context)
build_qsase_learning_rows(context)
build_qsase_repair_rows(context)
```

The check scripts should validate:

- View model is public-safe.
- View model has generated_at.
- Freshness state is present.
- Portfolio value time series is present or explicitly unavailable.
- Current portfolio rows are present or explicitly empty.
- Trading history rows are present or explicitly empty.
- Connected data source categories and source rows are present.
- Strategy universe includes overall strategies and strategies currently in
  play.
- Pattern lab separates linear and nonlinear pattern states.
- Trade intents are shown as intents, not trades or orders.
- Every card has artifact refs.
- Every decision record has state, reason, evidence, blocker, next action, and
  authority boundary.
- Strategy rows do not contain long prose.
- Overview has no detailed ledgers.
- Duplicate headlines are rejected.
- Duplicate safety copy is rejected.
- "Learned" requires attribution evidence.
- "Quantum" requires quantum review state.
- "Paper proof" requires paper proof ledger reference.
- "Trade-ready" requires PaperOps route evidence.
- Repair-worthy failures are not described as discipline.
- Live capital is false.
- Dashboard remains read-only.
- No order, broker write, Telegram command, or proof credit is created.

### 18.26 Dashboard Representation Summary

The QSASE dashboard is not a showcase for AI explanations. It is a system map
for operating Qadam.

It should show the full journey from source universe to trading universe,
pattern search, strategy hypothesis, Akber filtering, quantum review, shadow
testing, current trade intents, router decision, PaperOps state, paper account
reality, trading history, learning attribution, recursive improvement, and
repair. It should do that as compact decision records, line charts, and tables,
not as duplicated prose.

The user should always be able to answer:

```text
Is the paper portfolio going up or down?
What does Qadam currently hold?
What trades has it made?
What data sources are connected?
What strategies exist and which are currently in play?
What linear and nonlinear patterns is Qadam seeing?
What trades is Qadam thinking about making?
What does Qadam trust today?
Why?
What is blocking action?
What is the next allowed step?
Did anything actually change?
Is the boundary still paper-only and read-only?
```

If the dashboard cannot answer those questions quickly, it is not yet a QSASE
dashboard.

## 19. Telegram And Notification Boundary

The Telegram And Notification Boundary defines how QSASE communicates without
creating trading authority, notification spam, or unreadable generic messages.

It asks:

```text
What is important enough for Qadam to tell humans, how short should it be, and
how does the system prove that Telegram did not become a command path?
```

Telegram is a notification surface. It is not a strategy engine, an approval
surface, a broker route, a trade command interface, or a substitute for the
dashboard.

### 19.1 Purpose

The purpose of the Telegram boundary is to make Qadam's important state changes
visible without making them operationally dangerous or annoying.

Telegram should answer:

```text
What happened?
Why does it matter now?
What is blocked or next?
Was anything submitted?
Where should the human review details?
```

It should not answer with:

- a long system essay,
- a generic upgrade announcement,
- repeated boilerplate,
- a duplicated status block,
- a hidden command,
- an approval request,
- or a fake sense of certainty.

### 19.2 Core Principle

The core principle is:

```text
short, specific, review-only, command-disabled
```

Every QSASE Telegram message should be:

- Short enough to read on a phone.
- Specific enough to explain the actual state.
- Tied to a backend artifact.
- Deduped against recent messages.
- Public-safe.
- Review-only.
- Explicit that no unauthorized order or live-capital action occurred.

If a message needs six paragraphs to explain itself, it belongs on the
dashboard, not Telegram.

### 19.3 Telegram Roles

Telegram has two separate roles.

Inbound intake:

```text
Humans can send observations or context into Qadam.
```

Inbound intake is read-only. It may:

- Log world-event datapoints.
- Queue Research Analyst review.
- Add Strategy Lead considerations.
- Add source-context notes.
- Add dashboard review notes.

Inbound intake must not:

- Create commands.
- Create trade candidates.
- Create risk approvals.
- Create execution approvals.
- Create paper orders.
- Write brokers.
- Call quantum providers.
- Submit Q-CTRL jobs.
- Grant paper proof ledger credit.
- Enable live capital.

Outbound notification:

```text
Qadam can send short status notes to humans.
```

Outbound notification may report:

- strategy state,
- source or pattern state,
- why-not-trading-now,
- PaperOps gate state,
- paper-trial update,
- learning result,
- repair request,
- delivery failure,
- dashboard review pointer.

Outbound notification must not contain:

- trading commands,
- approval language,
- live instructions,
- broker payloads,
- secrets,
- raw credentials,
- operational prompts,
- proof claims unsupported by the paper proof ledger.

### 19.4 Message Classes

QSASE should support a small set of message classes.

Allowed classes:

- `qsase_strategy_state`
- `qsase_why_not_trading_now`
- `qsase_paperops_gate_update`
- `qsase_paper_trial_update`
- `qsase_learning_note`
- `qsase_repair_note`
- `qsase_source_coverage_note`
- `qsase_quantum_review_note`
- `qsase_dashboard_stale_note`
- `qsase_delivery_failure_note`

Disallowed classes:

- `trade_command`
- `approval_request`
- `broker_instruction`
- `live_capital_instruction`
- `manual_override`
- `proof_credit_grant`
- `qctrl_submit_instruction`
- `secret_or_credential_notice`
- `generic_system_update`
- `codebase_essay`

Codebase or deployment notifications, when allowed by a separate deployment
gate, should be treated as operational communication only. They should not be
mixed with QSASE strategy or PaperOps notifications.

### 19.5 Message Shape

Every QSASE Telegram message should fit this shape:

```text
Qadam [note type]
State: [specific state]
Reason: [specific reason]
Next: [next allowed review or repair step]
Order: none submitted / paper order already recorded by PaperOps
```

Optional fifth line:

```text
Review: dashboard
```

Most messages should be four or five lines. The body should not include
section headings such as "What changed", "Why it matters", or "What to check"
unless the message class explicitly requires a deployment-style note.

### 19.6 Strategy State Template

Allowed strategy note:

```text
Qadam strategy note
State: shadow-only
Reason: event odds moved, but market confirmation is weak
Next: keep watching until Akber filter passes
Order: none submitted
```

Allowed paper-review candidate note:

```text
Qadam strategy note
State: PaperOps review candidate
Reason: ACLED, Kalshi, and XLE line up
Next: PaperOps gate checks idempotency and exposure
Order: none submitted by QSASE
```

Rejected generic note:

```text
Qadam strategy engine active.
Advanced AI detected market opportunity.
Dashboard updated.
```

The rejected note is too vague, has no blocker, no evidence, no next action,
and implies more authority than it proves.

### 19.7 Why-Not-Trading Template

Allowed:

```text
Qadam no-trade note
State: no fresh eligible submit
Reason: duplicate exposure check is still active
Next: wait for exposure to clear or new distinct setup
Order: none submitted
```

Allowed:

```text
Qadam no-trade note
State: route hold
Reason: paper account mirror is stale
Next: repair mirror, then rerun PaperOps pass
Order: none submitted
```

Rejected:

```text
Qadam is waiting for optimal market conditions.
```

The rejected note hides whether Qadam is disciplined, blocked, broken, or
simply idle.

### 19.8 Paper-Trial Template

Paper-trial messages must preserve real calendar and proof boundaries.

Allowed:

```text
Qadam paper trial note
State: 30-day paper growth trial continues
Reason: paper proof ledger has new closed-trade evidence
Next: review dashboard trade state
```
