```text
Qadam has quantum-reviewed pattern recognition and quantum gate awareness, but
QSASE does not yet have a full nonlinear and quantum pattern lab over historical
source-price memory.
```

Gap:

- Nonlinear relationships are not yet systematically searched across the full
  universe.
- Quantum review usefulness is not yet attributed per pattern.
- Hardware, simulator, and classical fallback states are not yet normalized in
  one QSASE lab artifact.
- Quantum review is not yet linked to every strategy hypothesis that requires
  it.
- Nonlinear overfit controls are not yet standardized.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_nonlinear_quantum_pattern_lab`.
- Record nonlinear method, regime state, path dependence, quantum mode,
  Q-CTRL state, ambiguity score, and usefulness score.
- Validate that quantum review cannot become trade confirmation.

Boundary:

```text
Quantum review is required where specified, but it is still review. It is not
order authority.
```

### 23.9 Strategy Foundry Gap

Current state:

```text
Qadam has strategy families, strategy research intake, strategy lead context,
and proposal surfaces, but not a QSASE Strategy Foundry that turns full-universe
patterns into governed hypotheses.
```

Gap:

- Existing strategy artifacts do not yet synthesize new families from universal
  pattern search.
- Existing strategy records are not yet tied to a QSASE Research Goal lineage.
- Candidate identity and idempotency material are not yet created at the QSASE
  hypothesis layer.
- Rejected strategy hypotheses are not yet stored in one Foundry ledger.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_strategy_foundry`.
- Allow new strategy families as hypotheses only.
- Require source-price lineage, Research Goal lineage, candidate identity,
  paperability, invalidation, risk concept, and rejection reasons.

Boundary:

```text
A strategy hypothesis is not a trade candidate and not an order.
```

### 23.10 Akber Filter Backtest Gap

Current state:

```text
Akber's filter exists conceptually and operationally as a selectivity layer, but
QSASE does not yet backtest it across every strategy family and historical
candidate.
```

Gap:

- Akber filter outcomes are not yet replayed systematically across historical
  candidates.
- Filter pass, veto, and hold reasons are not yet normalized across every
  strategy hypothesis.
- Filter threshold improvement is not yet tied to attribution evidence.
- Filter strictness and looseness are not yet measurable over full-universe
  history.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_akber_filter_integration`.
- Record filter reason, market confirmation, veto logic, and threshold proposal
  state.
- Feed filter results to Strategy Router and Learning And Attribution Ledger.

Boundary:

```text
Akber filter pass is not execution approval. Akber filter changes are
proposal-only.
```

### 23.11 Shadow Strategy Simulator Gap

Current state:

```text
The existing shadow strategy runner is blocked or no-op unless learning
approval is present.
```

Gap:

- It is not yet upgraded into a QSASE historical and forward shadow simulator.
- Shadow strategy outcomes are not yet connected to full-universe pattern
  lineage.
- Counterfactual paper outcomes are not yet standardized.
- Shadow-to-learning and shadow-to-router transitions are not yet complete.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_shadow_strategy_simulator`.
- Support historical replay and forward watch.
- Record no-order state, counterfactual outcome, evidence class, and lineage.
- Feed learning attribution without creating proof.

Boundary:

```text
Shadow success cannot become a paper order or paper proof ledger credit.
```

### 23.12 Strategy Router Gap

Current state:

```text
Qadam has many review and gate artifacts, but not one QSASE router that assigns
every strategy hypothesis to an explicit next state.
```

Gap:

- No central QSASE router state machine exists yet.
- Why-not-trading-now is not yet produced by QSASE from strategy lineage.
- Repair-worthy failures are not yet separated from valid no-action states at
  the QSASE router layer.
- Router decisions are not yet connected to PaperOps handoff records.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_strategy_router`.
- Emit reject, watchlist, shadow-only, paper-review candidate, hold, repair, or
  blocked-safety-boundary states.
- Require exact reason and next allowed action.

Boundary:

```text
Router output is not a qualified setup, risk approval, execution approval, or
order.
```

### 23.13 PaperOps Handoff Gap

Current state:

```text
PaperOps exists and is guarded, but QSASE does not yet have a formal upstream
handoff interface into PaperOps review.
```

Gap:

- Router-approved strategy hypotheses are not yet converted into controlled
  PaperOps handoff records.
- QSASE handoff does not yet preserve Research Goal lineage, candidate
  identity, and idempotency material.
- Duplicate exposure, daily drawdown, Q-CTRL hold, paper account mirror, and
  guarded Alpaca Paper route state are not yet represented in a QSASE handoff
  artifact.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_paperops_gate_interface`.
- Hand off context only.
- Reject duplicate, unsafe, non-paperable, source-quorum-failed, or
  route-unavailable candidates before PaperOps.

Boundary:

```text
Existing PaperOps remains the only paper submit route. QSASE handoff cannot
submit.
```

### 23.14 Learning And Attribution Gap

Current state:

```text
Qadam has Phase 6 learning surfaces, model-weight proposals, trust-score
proposals, strategy-weight proposals, and architect recommendations, but QSASE
does not yet have one component attribution ledger spanning full-universe
patterns, shadow, paper, non-trades, rejections, and system defects.
```

Gap:

- Component attribution is not yet unified across source universe, models,
  quantum review, Akber Filter, router, PaperOps, risk, execution, and route
  state.
- Non-trades are not yet first-class QSASE learning records.
- Rejected hypotheses are not yet first-class QSASE learning records.
- System defects are not yet separated from strategy failures in one QSASE
  ledger.
- Strategy/source/model/filter proposals are not yet generated from QSASE
  attribution.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_learning_attribution_ledger`.
- Separate real paper, shadow, backtest, non-trade, rejected hypothesis, and
  system defect evidence.
- Keep proposals approval-gated and unapplied.

Boundary:

```text
Learning can propose. It cannot mutate strategy, model weights, source trust,
or proof.
```

### 23.15 Recursive Improvement Gap

Current state:

```text
Qadam has self-improvement proposals and repair-aware automation behavior, but
not one QSASE recursive cycle contract that connects discovery, tests, routing,
PaperOps outcomes, learning, proposals, and repairs.
```

Gap:

- No single recursive cycle artifact exists.
- No next-cycle instruction packet exists.
- No unified distinction exists between valid no-action, strategy rejection,
  route hold, and system repair across all QSASE layers.
- Self-healing is not yet integrated into QSASE's own cycle.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_recursive_improvement_contract`.
- Record cycle state, proposal state, repair queue, next-cycle focus, and
  authority flags.
- Preserve self-healing as narrow repo/runtime repair only.

Boundary:

```text
Recursive improvement cannot silently apply its own recommendations.
```

### 23.16 Dashboard Visibility Gap

Current state:

```text
The dashboard has a Mission Control flow and status mirrors, but it does not
yet expose QSASE as a compact self-aware strategy operating layer.
```

Gap:

- No QSASE dashboard view model exists.
- QSASE Snapshot is not yet rendered.
- Portfolio value line graph is not yet governed by a QSASE dashboard artifact.
- Current portfolio holdings are not yet rendered as QSASE-linked rows.
- Trading history is not yet connected to source packet, strategy, pattern, and
  paper proof ledger lineage.
- Connected data source categories and individual source rows are not yet
  rendered as one Source Intelligence Network.
- Strategy Universe does not yet show QSASE strategy hypothesis rows.
- Strategy Universe does not yet distinguish all strategies from strategies
  currently in play.
- Source and trading universe matrix is not yet visible.
- Linear and nonlinear pattern lab results are not yet visible as decision
  records.
- Trade intents are not yet visible as intents separate from trades, orders,
  approvals, and qualified setups.
- Learning attribution and recursive improvement are not yet visible as QSASE
  modules.
- Anti-slop checks for QSASE-specific cards do not yet exist.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_dashboard_view_model`.
- Build portfolio value, current portfolio, trading history, source network,
  strategy universe, pattern lab, and trade intent dashboard artifacts.
- Render compact decision records.
- Keep the portfolio value line graph, holdings, trading history, connected
  sources, strategy universe, pattern lab, and trade intents visible in the
  default dashboard.
- Keep detailed ledgers out of Overview.
- Add duplicate, generic, stale, and authority-drift checks.

Boundary:

```text
Dashboard can mirror QSASE. It cannot approve, submit, command, or grant
authority.
```

### 23.17 Telegram Notification Gap

Current state:

```text
Qadam has Telegram quality checks and notification artifacts, but QSASE does
not yet have its own short, deduped, strategy-aware notification boundary.
```

Gap:

- No QSASE Telegram message candidate artifact exists.
- No QSASE-specific dedupe ledger exists.
- No QSASE notification class taxonomy exists.
- Inbound read-only intake is not yet represented as a QSASE source-context
  artifact.
- Delivery failure is not yet integrated into QSASE Communications state.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_telegram_notification_boundary`.
- Add message classes, templates, quality checks, dedupe, delivery receipts,
  and dashboard Communications mirror.
- Keep inbound command-like messages ignored.

Boundary:

```text
Telegram can notify. It cannot command, approve, submit, or grant proof.
```

### 23.18 Certification Gap

Current state:

```text
Individual phases and modules have many checks, but QSASE does not yet have one
end-to-end certification bundle.
```

Gap:

- No `check_qsase_certification.py` exists.
- No end-to-end lineage check exists from source observation to dashboard and
  Telegram candidate.
- No single authority flag audit covers every QSASE artifact.
- No negative probe suite exists for QSASE.
- No certification status distinguishes shadow-only from PaperOps handoff
  readiness.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_end_to_end_certification`.
- Build `check_qsase_certification.py`.
- Add hard negative probes.
- Produce one certification artifact and one concise acceptance report.

Boundary:

```text
Certification can certify research and paper-review handoff readiness. It
cannot certify live capital.
```

### 23.19 Data Source Plugin Gaps

Current state:

```text
Qadam is designed to ingest many source families, but some high-value sources
may still be credential-gated, partially integrated, or not connected to QSASE
lineage.
```

Relevant source families:

- Kalshi.
- Reddit.
- Capitol Trades.
- Unusual Whales.
- Stock Act.
- prediction-market sources.
- logistics and physical activity sources.
- macroeconomic sources.
- market price sources.
- world event sources.

Gap:

- Plugged-in source status does not automatically mean historical coverage is
  complete.
- Source samples do not automatically mean source trust is earned.
- Source context does not automatically mean paperability exists.
- Prediction-market or social data does not automatically mean direct trading
  route exists.

Required closure:

- Represent every source family in the universal matrix.
- Record configured, missing, credential-gated, degraded, disabled, and
  read-only states.
- Connect source families to instrument mappings and strategy families.
- Keep source promotion proposal-first.

Boundary:

```text
New data sources expand observation. They do not expand execution authority.
```

### 23.20 Trading Universe Gaps

Current state:

```text
Qadam watches and reasons about multiple market expressions, but QSASE needs a
formal trading-universe registry that separates research expressions from
paperable instruments.
```

Gap:

- Not every researched market expression has a paperable instrument.
- Some source signals map indirectly to ETFs or equities.
- Some markets may be observable but not tradable through the current guarded
  route.
- Instrument mappings need horizon, liquidity, session, volatility, exposure,
  duplicate-conflict, and route-state fields.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_trading_universe`.
- Add paperability, route, session, exposure, and duplicate-conflict fields.
- Keep non-paperable markets research-only.

Boundary:

```text
Researchable is not paperable. Paperable is not submitted. Submitted only
occurs through existing PaperOps.
```

### 23.21 Governance Gaps

Current state:

```text
Qadam has many authority flags and proposal-only modules, but QSASE needs a
single governance contract that every new artifact inherits.
```

Gap:

- Universal QSASE authority flags are not yet implemented as one shared audit.
- Cross-artifact authority leaks are not yet checked in one certification
  bundle.
- Source promotion, strategy mutation, model-weight mutation, trust-score
  mutation, and proof boundary checks are not yet unified under QSASE.

Required closure:

- Build `qsase_governance_safety_contract`.
- Build universal authority audits.
- Add violation taxonomy and fail-closed behavior.

Boundary:

```text
Governance gaps should be closed by adding checks, not by loosening boundaries.
```

### 23.22 Non-Gaps: Intentional Boundaries

The following are not gaps:

- Strategy weight updates being proposal-only.
- Model weight updates being proposal-only.
- Trust-score updates being proposal-only.
- Source promotion requiring approval.
- Shadow strategy runner refusing to create orders.
- Historical backtests refusing to create proof.
- Telegram inbound being read-only.
- Dashboard being read-only.
- Q-CTRL holds blocking progress when required.
- Live capital being disabled.
- Paper proof ledger requiring real guarded paper lifecycle evidence.

These are safety properties. QSASE must preserve them.

### 23.23 Gap Closure Priority

Recommended priority:

1. Governance and self-model gaps.
2. Universal source-price matrix gaps.
3. Historical memory gaps.
4. Full-universe pattern search gaps.
5. Linear and nonlinear lab gaps.
6. Strategy Foundry and Akber Filter gaps.
7. Shadow and router gaps.
8. PaperOps handoff gap.
9. Learning attribution and recursive improvement gaps.
10. Dashboard and Telegram visibility gaps.
11. End-to-end certification gap.

This order mirrors the implementation phases. It avoids creating a strategy
engine before the evidence, safety, and validation spine exists.

### 23.24 Current Known Gaps Summary

The main gap is not that Qadam lacks intelligence. The main gap is that its
intelligence is distributed across current-state pattern recognition, edge
memory, strategy research intake, Phase 6 learning surfaces, PaperOps, dashboard
visibility, and Telegram notification modules.

QSASE must unify those pieces into one full-universe strategy operating system:
source-price matrix, historical memory, linear and nonlinear pattern labs,
quantum review, Strategy Foundry, Akber Filter, shadow simulation, Strategy
Router, PaperOps handoff, learning attribution, recursive improvement,
dashboard visibility, Telegram boundary, and end-to-end certification.

The bridge must be built without weakening what already protects Qadam: the
guarded PaperOps route, real calendar, paper proof ledger boundary, proposal-only
learning, read-only dashboard, read-only Telegram inbound, Q-CTRL holds, and
live-capital-off state.

## 24. Future Evolution

Future Evolution describes what QSASE can become after the first certified
implementation exists.

It asks:

```text
Once QSASE can safely understand itself, search the full source-price universe,
backtest patterns, create strategy hypotheses, route paper-review candidates,
learn from outcomes, and preserve all boundaries, how should Qadam keep
evolving?
```

The answer is not "turn on live trading." The answer is to deepen Qadam's
self-aware strategy intelligence while preserving the same proof, governance,
PaperOps, dashboard, Telegram, and live-capital boundaries that make the system
auditable.

### 24.1 Future Evolution Principle

The future evolution principle is:

```text
increase adaptive intelligence before increasing authority.
```

Qadam should become better at:

- seeing more sources,
- testing more relationships,
- rejecting more false edges,
- discovering more strategy families,
- understanding its own model stack,
- measuring quantum usefulness,
- selecting the right PaperOps handoffs,
- learning from non-trades,
- repairing its own broken artifacts,
- explaining itself to humans.

Qadam should not automatically become more authorized because it becomes more
intelligent.

### 24.2 Evolution Maturity Ladder

QSASE should mature through levels.

`Level 0: Documented Doctrine`

```text
QSASE exists as a governance-backed implementation plan.
```

`Level 1: Certified Self-Model`

```text
Qadam can explain its own stack, limits, source universe, trading universe, and
authority boundaries from runtime artifacts.
```

`Level 2: Full Source-Price Research`

```text
Qadam can build and refresh a universal source-price matrix and historical
memory layer.
```

`Level 3: Pattern Discovery`

```text
Qadam can run full-universe linear, nonlinear, and quantum-reviewed pattern
search.
```

`Level 4: Strategy Foundry`

```text
Qadam can convert validated patterns into strategy hypotheses and reject weak
or unsafe ideas.
```

`Level 5: Shadow And Paper Review Routing`

```text
Qadam can route strategies into shadow, watchlist, reject, repair, or upstream
PaperOps handoff states.
```

`Level 6: Learning Attribution`

```text
Qadam can attribute paper trades, non-trades, shadow outcomes, backtests,
rejections, and system defects to specific components.
```

`Level 7: Recursive Improvement`

```text
Qadam can propose improved strategy weights, source trust, model roles, filter
thresholds, router policies, and repair work without applying them silently.
```

`Level 8: Paper-Live Certification Support`

```text
Qadam can support paper-live certification evidence and readiness review while
still not enabling live capital.
```

`Level 9: Governed Authority Review`

```text
Any future live-capital discussion is handled by a separate governance process,
not by QSASE itself.
```

### 24.3 Richer Historical Datasets

QSASE's first version should prove the historical source-price memory contract.
Future versions should expand depth, breadth, and quality.

Future dataset improvements:

- Longer historical windows.
- Higher-frequency price data.
- Intraday event alignment.
- Options and volatility surfaces where permitted.
- Cross-asset futures proxies where permitted.
- Corporate event calendars.
- Earnings and guidance history.
- Sector and factor baskets.
- Supply-chain event timelines.
- Defense procurement timelines.
- Macro release calendars.
- Prediction-market odds histories.
- Social narrative histories.
- Congressional trade filing histories.
- Physical-world disruption histories.

Required controls:

- Provenance for every dataset.
- Coverage windows for every dataset.
- Missing-window records.
- Revision-history handling.
- Timezone normalization.
- No lookahead leakage.
- No survivorship bias where avoidable.
- No historical record converted into paper proof.

Future historical depth should improve research reliability, not alter paper
proof rules.

### 24.4 Data Source Evolution

QSASE should treat new data sources as source families that must pass
provenance, trust, coverage, and source-quorum rules.

Priority source evolutions:

- Kalshi / OddsPipe normalized prediction-market coverage.
- Polymarket read-only market context.
- Reddit retail attention and narrative saturation.
- Capitol Trades / STOCK Act filings.
- SEC EDGAR and corporate disclosure context.
- Options-flow and volatility context if re-selected.
- Logistics, AIS, aviation, satellite, and supply-chain context.
- Macro, rates, inflation, and central-bank data.
- Physical risk sources such as conflict, weather, fires, earthquakes, and
  infrastructure outages.
- Developer, patent, and technology trend data.

Rules:

- New source does not mean new authority.
- Credential-configured does not mean trusted.
- Trusted does not mean canonical.
- Canonical does not mean sufficient alone.
- Source quorum remains required.
- Prediction-market data remains context unless a separate governed route
  exists.
- Social data cannot satisfy source quorum by itself.
- Filings data must be cross-validated against price, sector, and timing.

Each future source should enter QSASE through the universal source-price matrix,
not through one-off strategy code.

### 24.5 Trading Universe Evolution

QSASE's trading universe should expand carefully.

Future trading universe categories:

- Equity single names.
- Equity sector ETFs.
- Index ETFs.
- Commodity ETFs.
- Bond and rates ETFs.
- Volatility-linked instruments where permitted.
- Defense and aerospace baskets.
- Semiconductor and AI infrastructure baskets.
- Energy and shipping proxies.
- Precious metals proxies.
- Macro-sensitive baskets.
- Event-sensitive baskets.
- Future governed prediction-market expressions.
- Future governed crypto or perps research expressions if selected.

Each instrument or market expression should have:

- observable price data,
- historical coverage,
- liquidity state,
- session state,
- paperability state,
- route state,
- risk profile,
- duplicate exposure profile,
- source relationships,
- strategy-family relationships,
- proof eligibility state.

Rules:

- Researchable is not paperable.
- Paperable is not submitted.
- Submitted only occurs through existing guarded PaperOps.
- Future venue expansion requires separate governance.
- Future live venue expansion requires an entirely separate live-capital process.

### 24.6 Prediction-Market Evolution

Prediction-market data is especially valuable to QSASE because it can represent
probability repricing before traditional markets move.

Future uses:

- Event probability lag detection.
- Cross-market probability spreads.
- Election-sensitive basket research.
- Macro-release probability surfaces.
- Conflict or policy risk repricing.
- Prediction-market odds against ETF, sector, and single-name reactions.

Allowed first direction:

```text
read-only probability context feeding source-price pattern search.
```

Possible future governed direction:

```text
paper or simulation review of prediction-market-specific strategies, only if
separate route authority, risk policy, compliance review, and execution controls
exist.
```

Forbidden shortcut:

```text
prediction-market odds create trade authority.
```

QSASE can discover that prediction-market odds are useful. It cannot turn that
usefulness into venue write authority.

### 24.7 Reddit And Narrative Evolution

Reddit and broader social narrative data should be used as attention,
saturation, and edge-decay context.

Future uses:

- Retail attention spikes.
- Crowd narrative saturation.
- Meme-risk detection.
- Semiconductor and AI infrastructure chatter.
- Crypto and risk-appetite context where relevant.
- Options and volatility narrative overlays.
- Contrarian exhaustion signals.

Controls:

- Social data should be supplemental unless cross-validated.
- Social data should not satisfy source quorum alone.
- Social data should be normalized for spam and bot-like behavior.
- Narrative spikes should be tested against price, volatility, and options
  data.
- Strategy hypotheses should record whether social data helped, hurt, or added
  noise.

Social data can be useful, but it is often noisy. QSASE should use it to test
attention dynamics, not to chase posts.

### 24.8 Capitol Trades And Filings Evolution

Capitol Trades / STOCK Act and related filings can become a powerful source of
slow-moving information asymmetry research.

Future uses:

- Congressional trading clusters.
- Defense procurement sensitivity.
- Policy-sensitive sector baskets.
- Disclosure timing versus price drift.
- Filing clusters against SEC, contracts, patents, and news.
- Single-name and sector underreaction studies.

Controls:

- Filing timestamp and transaction timestamp must be separated.
- Reporting delay must be modeled.
- Congressional trade data must be cross-validated.
- Individual filings should not create direct trade candidates.
- Strategy hypotheses should use baskets or risk-controlled expressions where
  more appropriate than single names.

QSASE should treat filings as slow, delayed, legally available signals with
context risk, not as instant signals.

### 24.9 Strategy Family Evolution

QSASE should eventually support more strategy families than the initial ones,
but new families must be earned.

Possible future families:

- Event probability lag.
- Narrative saturation reversal.
- Capitol filing underreaction.
- Supply-chain disruption drift.
- Macro release repricing.
- Physical risk to commodity proxy.
- Defense procurement momentum.
- Semiconductor attention-volatility spread.
- Cross-source disagreement arbitrage.
- Source-quorum decay avoidance.
- Volatility compression before event repricing.
- Prediction-market probability spread research.

New family requirements:

- source-price pattern lineage,
- historical evidence,
- out-of-sample test,
- nonlinear or quantum review where useful,
- Akber Filter result,
- shadow replay result,
- paperability assessment,
- risk concept,
- invalidation concept,
- router decision,
- governance review before active use.

A new family begins as a hypothesis. It does not become active because it is
interesting.

### 24.10 Recursive Strategy Discovery Evolution

QSASE should eventually become better at asking:

```text
Which strategies fit my own architecture best?
```

That means strategy discovery should become self-aware of:

- which sources Qadam observes best,
- which sources are stale or degraded,
- which models add useful critique,
- which model outputs add noise,
- where quantum review changes decisions,
- where Akber's filter blocks false positives,
- where PaperOps route state limits action,
- where paper lifecycle evidence is strongest,
- where dashboard visibility improves human review,
- where Telegram should stay silent.

The future goal is not just to find profitable-looking patterns. It is to find
patterns that Qadam can observe, test, route, explain, paper-trade, and learn
from better than other systems.

### 24.11 Backtesting Evolution

QSASE's backtesting should evolve from simple validation into a research lab.

Future capabilities:

- Multi-source walk-forward testing.
- Cross-validation across regimes.
- Event-study factories.
- Feature-ablation tests.
- Source-ablation tests.
- Model-ablation tests.
- Akber Filter ablation.
- Quantum-review usefulness ablation.
- Strategy-family clustering.
- Portfolio interaction tests.
- Duplicate exposure simulations.
- Drawdown stress tests.
- No-trade decision backtests.
- Rejected hypothesis after-action checks.

Backtesting should become a way to falsify strategy ideas, not only to sell
them.

### 24.12 Quantum Evolution

Quantum tooling should evolve from gate-awareness into measured usefulness.

Future improvements:

- Better nonlinear feature encoding.
- Better ambiguity scoring.
- Better regime boundary tests.
- Clearer simulator versus hardware status.
- Q-CTRL readiness evidence where required.
- Hardware access when explicitly approved and available.
- Quantum-review usefulness attribution.
- Quantum ablation against classical nonlinear methods.
- Quantum cost, latency, and reliability tracking.

Rules:

- Quantum hardware access is not trade authority.
- Quantum review is not confirmation.
- Quantum usefulness must be measured against decisions.
- Quantum output must remain one review input among several.
- Q-CTRL holds remain binding where required.

The right future question is not:

```text
Did quantum say trade?
```

It is:

```text
Did quantum review help Qadam avoid a false edge or identify a relationship
that classical review missed?
```

### 24.13 Model Stack Evolution

QSASE should improve how the local model, frontier model, deterministic Python
code, and quantum layer collaborate.

Future model roles:

- Local model handles triage, compression, source categorization, and quick
  contradiction detection.
- Frontier model handles challenge, synthesis, failure-mode review, and
  explanation quality.
- Python orchestrator handles deterministic validation, artifact writing,
  authority checks, and route decisions.
- Quantum layer handles nonlinear ambiguity review where useful.

Future improvements:

- Track when Gemma compressed away useful information.
- Track when Gemini over-narrated weak evidence.
- Track when deterministic code blocked a model hallucination.
- Track when quantum review changed routing.
- Track model-specific contribution in learning attribution.
- Propose model-weight changes without applying them.

The future model stack should become more measured, not more magical.

### 24.14 PaperOps Evolution

QSASE should make PaperOps better supplied, not less guarded.

Future PaperOps improvements:

- Cleaner upstream Research Goal lineage.
- More distinct candidate identities.
- Better idempotency seed generation.
- Stronger duplicate exposure context.
- Better source quorum packets.
- Better Akber Filter packets.
- Better quantum review packets.
- Better why-not-trading-now reasons.
- Better non-trade learning records.
- Better closed-trade attribution.

PaperOps should remain responsible for:

- Signal Integrity.
- Strategy Lead review.
- Head of Quant review where required.
- Risk Agent sizing.
- Execution Policy.
- Idempotency enforcement.
- Q-CTRL paper consultation holds.
- Guarded Alpaca Paper route.
- Paper lifecycle polling.
- Paper proof ledger review.

QSASE should never become the submitter.

### 24.15 Paper-Live Certification Support

QSASE can eventually support paper-live certification by improving evidence
quality.

It can help show:

- which strategies have repeated paper-review quality,
- which strategies have real paper lifecycle evidence,
- which strategies have clean non-trade discipline,
- which sources helped and which added noise,
- which model roles helped and which hurt,
- which quantum reviews were useful,
- which PaperOps holds were safety-valid,
- which route defects were repaired,
- which learning proposals remain unapplied.

It cannot:

- grant paper-live certification by itself,
- bypass PaperOps,
- bypass Q-CTRL,
- bypass risk,
- bypass execution policy,
- bypass proof rules,
- enable live capital.

Paper-live certification support is evidence support, not authority.

### 24.16 Live-Capital Discussion Boundary

Any future live-capital discussion must remain outside QSASE's authority.

Before any live-capital discussion, Qadam would need separate evidence and
governance that is not created by QSASE alone:

- sustained real paper lifecycle evidence,
- clean paper proof ledger review,
- independent governance approval,
- legal and compliance review,
- broker live credential policy,
- live risk limits,
- capital allocation policy,
- kill-switch policy,
- incident response policy,
- audit and rollback process,
- human approval workflow,
- production monitoring,
- live notification gate,
- explicit live-capital charter.

QSASE may inform such a discussion. It cannot start it, approve it, or satisfy
it alone.

### 24.17 Dashboard Evolution

The dashboard should evolve from status mirror into strategy operating map.

Future dashboard improvements:

- Source universe map.
- Trading universe map.
- Source-price matrix view.
- Strategy family atlas.
- Pattern lab timeline.
- Quantum usefulness panel.
- Akber Filter attribution panel.
- Non-trade learning panel.
- PaperOps handoff lineage view.
- Recursive improvement cycle view.
- Governance review queue.
- Certification maturity ladder.

Dashboard rules remain:

- one global Safety Status,
- no duplicate prose,
- no generic AI claims,
- no stale state presented as current,
- no command controls,
- no approval controls,
- no broker controls,
- no live-capital implication.

The dashboard should become more legible, not more verbose.

### 24.18 Telegram Evolution

Telegram should evolve toward fewer, sharper messages.

Future improvements:

- Better semantic duplicate suppression.
- Better per-message evidence references.
- Better repair-note clarity.
- Better paper-trial update thresholds.
- Better learning-note specificity.
- Better notification cadence rules.
- Better separation of codebase deploy notes from trading-state notes.
- Better delivery failure mirroring.

Telegram should not evolve into:

- a command channel,
- an approval interface,
- a strategy chat bot,
- a broker interface,
- a long-form dashboard replacement.

The best Telegram future is quieter and more useful.

### 24.19 Self-Healing Evolution

Self-healing should evolve from repair prompts into a narrow, auditable repair
system.

Future self-healing capabilities:

- Detect stale runtime artifacts.
- Detect missing source coverage.
- Detect schema drift.
- Detect dashboard mirror mismatch.
- Detect Telegram delivery failure.
- Detect PaperOps validation failure.
- Detect missing Q-CTRL state.
- Detect historical memory gaps.
- Queue repair prompts.
- Run approved validation commands.
- Record repair outcomes.
- Distinguish repaired, still blocked, and human-governance-required states.

Self-healing must not:

- edit secrets,
- load live credentials,
- enable live capital,
- force trades,
- bypass PaperOps,
- bypass Q-CTRL,
- mutate strategy,
- grant proof.

Future self-healing is operational hygiene, not autonomous trading authority.

### 24.20 Governance Evolution

Governance should evolve from false flags into a full review workflow.

Future governance capabilities:

- Strategy proposal review queue.
- Source promotion review queue.
- Model-weight proposal review queue.
- Trust-score proposal review queue.
- Akber threshold proposal review queue.
- Router policy review queue.
- PaperOps gate proposal review queue.
- Dashboard and Telegram wording review queue.
- Repair escalation review queue.
- Rollback plans for applied changes.
- Audit trail for approved apply modules.

Even then, QSASE should remain proposal-first. Applying approved changes should
be handled by separate modules with their own audit and rollback contracts.

### 24.21 Research Goal Evolution

Research Goals should become the connective tissue of future QSASE.

Future Research Goal fields:

- source pattern being tested,
- trading universe expression,
- strategy family,
- hypothesis owner,
- evidence class,
- backtest windows,
- quantum review requirement,
- Akber Filter requirement,
- shadow requirement,
- PaperOps handoff eligibility,
- learning attribution state,
- dashboard visibility state,
- governance review state.

Every future strategy should be traceable back to a Research Goal. If it cannot
be traced, it should not be routed.

### 24.22 Portfolio-Level Evolution

QSASE should eventually reason about strategy interactions, not only isolated
setups.

Future portfolio research:

- Strategy-family correlation.
- Source-family correlation.
- Exposure clustering.
- Event concentration.
- Sector concentration.
- Macro regime concentration.
- Duplicate exposure across distinct Research Goals.
- Paper account drawdown stress.
- Open-order congestion.
- Non-trade opportunity cost.
- Strategy family contribution to paper outcomes.

Portfolio-level intelligence should improve PaperOps context. It should not
override Risk Agent sizing or Execution Policy.

### 24.23 Human Collaboration Evolution

QSASE should improve how humans review it.

Future collaboration improvements:

- Better dashboard review packets.
- Better Fund Manager comments integration.
- Better governance review diffs.
- Better "why Qadam held" explanations.
- Better "why Qadam rejected" explanations.
- Better "what changed since last run" summaries.
- Better evidence bundles for strategy proposals.
- Better plain-English explanations without losing auditability.

Human review should become more informed, not more burdened.

### 24.24 Future Roadmap Artifacts

Future evolution should be tracked through roadmap artifacts.

Recommended artifacts:

```text
data/runtime/qsase_capability_maturity_model.json
data/runtime/qsase_future_evolution_roadmap.json
data/runtime/qsase_source_expansion_candidates.json
data/runtime/qsase_trading_universe_expansion_candidates.json
data/runtime/qsase_strategy_family_expansion_candidates.json
data/runtime/qsase_quantum_usefulness_roadmap.json
data/runtime/qsase_paper_live_certification_support.json
data/runtime/qsase_live_capital_separation_audit.json
```

Recommended checks:

```text
scripts/check_qsase_future_evolution_roadmap.py
scripts/check_qsase_capability_maturity_model.py
scripts/check_qsase_live_capital_separation.py
```

These artifacts should describe evolution without granting authority.

### 24.25 Future Evolution Anti-Patterns

QSASE should avoid these future failure modes:

- Treating more data as more truth.
- Treating more model output as more certainty.
- Treating quantum review as magic confirmation.
- Treating better backtests as proof.
- Treating shadow success as paper success.
- Treating dashboard green status as trading authority.
- Treating Telegram clarity as command authority.
- Treating self-healing as permission to bypass gates.
- Treating paper-live certification support as live-capital approval.
- Treating new source integration as source promotion.
- Treating strategy discovery as strategy activation.

Each anti-pattern should have a validation probe before QSASE is expanded.

### 24.26 Long-Term Vision

The long-term vision is:

```text
Qadam continuously discovers which strategies best fit its own architecture,
data sources, models, quantum review layer, PaperOps route, and proof standard.
```

This means Qadam does not merely ask:

```text
What strategy looks profitable?
```

It asks:

```text
What strategy can I observe well, test honestly, route safely, paper-trade
through existing guards, explain clearly, learn from accurately, and improve
without lying to myself?
```

That is the essence of QSASE. It is not sentience. It is not a generic trading
AI. It is Qadam reflecting on its own 1s and 0s, its own source coverage, its
own model limits, its own quantum tools, its own PaperOps route, its own
dashboard and Telegram surfaces, and its own paper proof ledger to decide how
it can best fulfill its purpose.

### 24.27 Future Evolution Summary

Future QSASE evolution should make Qadam deeper, not looser.

It can expand historical datasets, source coverage, strategy families,
backtesting, nonlinear review, quantum usefulness measurement, PaperOps handoff
quality, learning attribution, dashboard clarity, Telegram precision,
self-healing, and governance review.

But every future step must preserve the core contract:

- paper-only until separate governance says otherwise,
- no live capital,
- no broker writes by QSASE,
- no Telegram command path,
- no dashboard authority,
- no LLM or quantum approval,
- no unapproved strategy mutation,
- no source promotion shortcut,
- no simulated paper-trial time,
- no paper proof ledger credit outside real guarded paper lifecycle evidence.

QSASE's future is not to make Qadam reckless. Its future is to make Qadam
increasingly accurate about what it is, what it can know, what it can test, what
it should reject, what it should route to PaperOps, and what it still must not
do.

## Appendix A. Operational Phase 0: PaperOps Execution Reliability Baseline

This appendix converts the earlier iterative PaperOps fix plan into a QSASE
readiness baseline.

Its purpose is practical:

```text
Before Qadam evolves into QSASE, prove that the existing paper-operation loop is
fresh, observable, lineage-complete, lifecycle-aware, and dashboard-consistent.
```

Phase 0 is not a separate product and not a competing architecture. It is the
operational floor beneath QSASE. If Phase 0 is unhealthy, QSASE may still run
research, backtests, shadow simulations, and dashboard explanations, but it
should not treat PaperOps handoff as reliable.

### A.1 Phase 0 Rule

The Phase 0 rule is:

```text
Do not ask QSASE to route better paper candidates until PaperOps can prove it
can see fresh opportunities, distinguish new setups, explain lifecycle state,
preserve lineage, and publish one consistent dashboard truth.
```

This rule protects QSASE from misdiagnosing operational failures as strategy
weakness.

Examples:

- If the scanner is stale, QSASE may think no edge exists when Qadam simply did
  not look recently enough.
- If candidate identity is weak, QSASE may think the market is empty when
  idempotency is suppressing duplicate-shaped records.
- If accepted orders have ambiguous lifecycle state, QSASE cannot learn whether
  the strategy failed, the broker route failed, or the order is still waiting.
- If proof lineage is broken, real paper lifecycle evidence cannot graduate
  into the paper proof ledger.
- If dashboard telemetry disagrees across artifacts, the Fund Manager cannot
  tell whether Qadam is idle, degraded, blocked, or correctly waiting.

### A.2 Relationship To The Main QSASE Plan

Phase 0 maps into the main QSASE plan as follows:

| Phase 0 Concern | QSASE Landing Zone | Why It Matters |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Fresh opportunity scanner | QSASE Self-Model, Universal Source-Price Matrix, Full-Universe Pattern Search | QSASE needs fresh source and market state before judging strategy readiness. |
| Fresh eligible candidate identity | Strategy Foundry, Strategy Router, PaperOps Gate Interface | Distinct setups need distinct thesis, lineage, invalidation, risk, and idempotency material. |
| Pending fills and lifecycle state | PaperOps Gate Interface, Learning And Attribution Ledger | QSASE cannot learn from trades whose order lifecycle is ambiguous. |
| Learning loop and validated edge | Linear Lab, Nonlinear Lab, Strategy Foundry, Learning Ledger | Candidate patterns need evidence before becoming strategy hypotheses. |
| Proof ledger lineage | PaperOps Gate Interface, Learning Ledger, Governance Contract | Real guarded paper lifecycle evidence must be traceable before proof credit is considered. |
| Telemetry consistency | Self-Model, Dashboard View Model, Certification | Public status must have one coherent runtime contract. |
| Deploy and repo hygiene | Dashboard View Model, Telegram Boundary, Certification | Dashboard claims must match deployed runtime mirrors. |
| Review signatures | Self-Model, Akber Filter, Strategy Router, PaperOps Gate Interface | Model and quantum reviews can qualify or block handoff, but cannot create broker authority. |

Phase 0 therefore becomes the reliability preflight for the 24-part QSASE
evolution.

### A.3 Phase 0 Output Artifacts

Phase 0 should produce one summary artifact and several focused support
artifacts.

Primary artifact:

```text
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_paperops_reliability_baseline.json
```

Support artifacts:

```text
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_scanner_freshness.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_candidate_identity_audit.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_paper_lifecycle_audit.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_validated_edge_readiness.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_proof_lineage_audit.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_telemetry_consistency.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_dashboard_deploy_hygiene.json
data/runtime/qsase_phase0_review_signature_readiness.json
```

Primary check:

```text
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_paperops_reliability_baseline.py
```

Focused checks:

```text
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_scanner_freshness.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_candidate_identity.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_paper_lifecycle.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_validated_edge_readiness.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_proof_lineage.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_telemetry_consistency.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_dashboard_deploy_hygiene.py
scripts/check_qsase_phase0_review_signature_readiness.py
```

These checks should be included in `scripts/check_qsase_certification.py` once
the appendix is implemented.

### A.4 Phase 0 Status Model

The baseline should use explicit statuses:

```text
ready
ready_with_gaps
degraded
blocked
not_implemented
```

Status meanings:

- `ready`: PaperOps reliability is strong enough for QSASE handoff dependency.
- `ready_with_gaps`: QSASE may run, but named gaps must appear in dashboard and
  certification.
- `degraded`: QSASE may research and shadow-test, but PaperOps handoff should
  be treated as unreliable.
- `blocked`: A hard reliability failure prevents QSASE certification.
- `not_implemented`: The check does not yet exist and cannot be assumed ready.

No Phase 0 status should imply live-capital readiness.

### A.5 Fresh Opportunity Scanner Baseline

The earlier plan correctly identified the scanner as a full-potential limit.
QSASE cannot find fresh setups if Qadam's opportunity scanner is stale.

Requirement:

```text
The opportunity scanner must run fresh on a local Mac scheduler, separate from
the hourly guarded submit runner.
```

Acceptance:

```text
During market hours, the scanner artifact age is under 25 minutes.
```

Artifact fields:

```text
scanner_status
scanner_scheduler_status
scanner_artifact_path
scanner_generated_at
scanner_artifact_age_seconds
market_hours_state
last_success_at
last_failure_at
failure_category
freshness_sla_seconds
freshness_sla_passed
```

QSASE behavior:

- If scanner freshness passes, QSASE may use scanner output as current context.
- If scanner freshness fails, QSASE must mark current opportunity visibility as
  degraded.
- Scanner freshness failure is not a strategy failure.
- Scanner freshness failure cannot force trades.

Dashboard wording:

```text
Opportunity scanner fresh: last scan 12 minutes ago.
```

or:

```text
Opportunity scanner stale: last scan 47 minutes ago, so Qadam may be missing
fresh setups.
```

### A.6 Fresh Eligible Candidate Identity Baseline

The earlier plan correctly identified duplicate and idempotency-held setups as
a major reason Qadam can appear unable to trade.

Requirement:

```text
Every candidate considered for paper review must carry a strong new-setup
identity.
```

Candidate identity fields:

```text
candidate_id
research_goal_id
thesis_id
source_packet_id
source_family_set
instrument
venue
time_window
setup_timestamp
invalidation_condition
risk_state
exposure_group
idempotency_namespace
idempotency_seed
idempotency_key
duplicate_exposure_state
duplicate_idempotency_state
```

Acceptance:

```text
The dashboard clearly says either that a fresh eligible candidate exists or the
exact reason no trade can be made.
```

Required no-trade reasons:

- no fresh scanner output,
- no source quorum,
- no distinct candidate identity,
- duplicate idempotency key,
- duplicate exposure conflict,
- risk budget unavailable,
- drawdown or kill-switch hold,
- quantum or Q-CTRL review hold,
- route readiness failure,
- broker mirror stale,
- market closed,
- lifecycle ambiguity,
- no PaperOps handoff allowed.

QSASE behavior:

- Strategy Foundry may propose a hypothesis without candidate identity.
- Strategy Router may not emit a PaperOps handoff without candidate identity.
- PaperOps Gate Interface must reject missing or duplicate idempotency material.

### A.7 Pending Fills And Lifecycle Baseline

The old plan's lifecycle concern remains important. A paper order that sits in
accepted, open, partially filled, rejected, canceled, or unknown state without
explanation contaminates learning.

Requirement:

```text
Every accepted or open Alpaca paper order must have an explicit lifecycle
explanation and next action.
```

Lifecycle policy states:

```text
continue_waiting
cancel_replace_review
cancel_requested
marked_no_action
filled
partially_filled
rejected
expired
unknown_needs_repair
```

Acceptance:

```text
No paper order sits in ambiguous state without a lifecycle explanation.
```

Artifact fields:

```text
paper_order_id
research_goal_id
candidate_id
submitted_at
broker_status
mirror_status
lifecycle_age_seconds
lifecycle_policy_state
next_allowed_action
why_not_closed
why_not_replaced
poller_status
repair_required
proof_eligible_after_close
```

QSASE behavior:

- Lifecycle ambiguity blocks proof-ledger promotion.
- Lifecycle ambiguity discounts learning attribution.
- Lifecycle ambiguity appears in dashboard and certification.
- QSASE cannot cancel, replace, or submit orders directly; it can only request
  PaperOps lifecycle review through existing guarded mechanisms.

### A.8 Learning Loop And Validated Edge Baseline

The old plan's candidate-edge graduation concept is directly relevant to QSASE.
It should become the bridge between raw pattern discovery and Strategy Foundry.

Requirement:

```text
Candidate patterns must become scored edge records before they can graduate
into validated strategy hypotheses.
```

Minimum evidence types:

- repeated lead-lag behavior,
- repeated divergence then convergence,
- source-before-price sequence,
- multi-source confirmation,
- volatility-adjusted response,
- drawdown-aware expectancy,
- regime-specific robustness,
- forward-window outcome stability,
- leakage-safe historical replay.

Acceptance:

```text
candidate_edges_under_observation can graduate to validated_edge only with
evidence.
```

Artifact fields:

```text
edge_id
source_relationship_id
instrument
pattern_family
sample_count
regime_count
lead_lag_score
divergence_score
source_before_price_score
linear_score
nonlinear_score
quantum_review_state
overfit_risk
leakage_check_state
validated_edge_state
graduation_reason
rejection_reason
```

QSASE behavior:

- Full-Universe Pattern Search creates candidate patterns.
- Linear and Nonlinear labs score them.
- Strategy Foundry can only consume accepted or watchlisted edge records.
- Weak, overfit, unsafe, or non-paperable edges become rejection records.

### A.9 Proof Ledger Lineage Baseline

The old plan's lineage chain is essential. Without it, Qadam may submit paper
orders but fail to convert closed paper trades into proof-ledger evidence.

Requirement:

```text
research_goal_id must survive from initial observation through candidate,
review, handoff, order, fill, close, postmortem, and proof review.
```

Required lineage chain:

```text
source_observation
-> research_goal_id
-> strategy_hypothesis_id
-> candidate_id
-> review_signature_set
-> risk_review_id
-> paperops_handoff_id
-> staged_order_id
-> submitted_order_id
-> broker_order_id
-> fill_id
-> position_id
-> close_event_id
-> postmortem_id
-> paper_proof_ledger_review_id
```

Acceptance:

```text
Closed paper trades can become paper proof ledger records when, and only when,
real guarded paper lifecycle evidence and full lineage exist.
```

Historical repair:

```text
Backfill lineage for old trades where possible, but never backfill paper-trial
time and never grant proof credit without real guarded lifecycle evidence.
```

QSASE behavior:

- Missing lineage blocks proof-ledger eligibility.
- Partial lineage can be preserved as learning context.
- Backfilled lineage must be marked as reconstructed.
- Reconstructed lineage is not simulated paper proof.

### A.10 Telemetry Consistency Baseline

The old plan's telemetry concern is still relevant. QSASE should not tolerate
one artifact reporting connected sources while another reports zero sources
unless the discrepancy is explicitly explained.

Requirement:

```text
Cockpit status must be the single public dashboard contract, and source counts
must reconcile across source registry, PaperOps, RS artifacts, and dashboard
view-model artifacts.
```

Acceptance:

```text
Dashboard, RS artifacts, PaperOps, and source registry agree, or the dashboard
shows the exact reconciliation gap.
```

Consistency fields:

```text
cockpit_status_generated_at
source_registry_generated_at
paperops_summary_generated_at
qsase_dashboard_generated_at
source_count_cockpit
source_count_registry
source_count_paperops
source_count_qsase
connected_source_count_delta
stale_artifact_count
reconciliation_status
reconciliation_gap_reasons
```

QSASE behavior:

- Telemetry mismatch is a certification failure unless explicitly degraded.
- Dashboard must not hide contradictory source counts.
- Telegram summaries should not report source counts if telemetry is degraded.

### A.11 Deploy And Repo Hygiene Baseline

The old plan's deploy hygiene requirement belongs in Phase 0 because dashboard
truth is part of operational reliability.

Requirement:

```text
Dashboard status mirrors must be committed and deployed after runtime refreshes
before Qadam claims the public dashboard is live-current.
```

Acceptance:

```text
qadam.trade reflects the latest runtime state, or the dashboard says it is stale
and names the stale mirror.
```

Check fields:

```text
dashboard_repo_dirty
runtime_status_mirror_changed
signature_mirror_changed
last_export_at
last_deploy_at
production_alias_updated
public_dashboard_checked_at
public_dashboard_fresh
stale_mirror_reason
```

QSASE behavior:

- Dashboard freshness cannot change trading authority.
- Dashboard staleness must be visible.
- Deploy failure must not be represented as strategy failure.
- Telegram may report dashboard staleness only as a notification, not a command.

### A.12 Review Signature Baseline

The old plan described Local LLM, Frontier LLM, Quantum, Risk, and Python COO
paper authority. QSASE should preserve the useful coordination model while
renaming it to avoid authority confusion.

In QSASE, these are review signatures, not independent broker authorities.

Roles:

| Role | Allowed Phase 0 Function | Not Allowed |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Local LLM | Promote fresh observations into research goals or reject weak observations. | Cannot see broker secrets, submit orders, or bypass PaperOps. |
| Frontier LLM | Challenge narrative, evidence quality, risk assumptions, and alternative explanations. | Cannot create broker authority or override hard gates. |
| Quantum layer | Co-sign, downgrade, or hold nonlinear pattern review; classical fallback must be labelled. | Cannot create trades, submit orders, or be treated as magic confirmation. |
| Risk Agent | Approve, reduce, or reject sizing and exposure. | Cannot bypass source quorum, idempotency, or route readiness. |
| Python COO | Submit only through existing guarded PaperOps when all gates pass. | Cannot submit outside the guarded paper route. |

Review signature contract:

```json
{
  "trade_intent_id": "ti-example",
  "research_goal_id": "rg-example",
  "candidate_id": "cand-example",
  "local_llm_review_state": "signed",
  "frontier_llm_review_state": "signed",
  "quantum_review_state": "signed_or_classical_fallback_labelled",
  "risk_review_state": "approved",
  "akber_filter_state": "passed",
  "source_quorum_state": "passed",
  "idempotency_state": "distinct",
  "duplicate_exposure_state": "clear",
  "paperops_handoff_allowed": true,
  "paper_submit_authority": "paperops_only",
  "broker_write_actor": "python_coo_via_guarded_paperops_only",
  "live_capital_enabled": false
}
```

Acceptance:

```text
Every submitted paper trade has the required review signatures or an explicit
documented bypass rule accepted by governance, while broker writes remain
Python COO via guarded PaperOps only.
```

QSASE behavior:

- A missing review signature may block PaperOps handoff.
- A negative review signature must block PaperOps handoff unless governance
  defines a narrower exception.
- Review signatures cannot create paper orders.
- Review signatures cannot grant paper proof ledger credit.

### A.13 Guarded Paper Submission Baseline

The old plan's final paper path remains valid when translated into QSASE-safe
terms.

Allowed path:

```text
source quorum
-> local LLM review
-> frontier LLM review where required
-> quantum or labelled fallback review where required
-> Akber filter
-> risk review
-> idempotency and duplicate exposure check
-> guarded PaperOps handoff
-> existing guarded Alpaca Paper route
-> lifecycle poll
-> postmortem
-> paper proof ledger review
```

Rules:

- Multiple paper-review candidates per day are allowed when each setup is
  distinct.
- Multiple paper trades per day are allowed only through the guarded Alpaca
  Paper route after all required gates pass.
- No forced trades.
- No live capital.
- No dashboard or Telegram broker commands.
- No unmanaged broker writes.
- No LLM direct broker credentials.
- No quantum direct broker credentials.
- No paper proof ledger credit before real guarded paper lifecycle evidence.

Acceptance:

```text
Qadam can make multiple paper trades per day when valid distinct setups exist,
but every submitted order must pass through the same guarded PaperOps route.
```

### A.14 Dashboard Phase 0 Baseline

The old plan's dashboard requirements should be implemented as compact decision
records, not verbose status prose.

Mission Control should show:

- portfolio value line graph,
- current portfolio holdings,
- trading history,
- which sources are connected,
- which data source categories are connected,
- whether scanner freshness passes,
- what Qadam is thinking about,
- which trade intents exist,
- which review signatures are present or missing,
- whether Local LLM, Frontier LLM, Quantum, Akber, Risk, and COO states permit
  handoff,
- whether idempotency is holding a duplicate,
- whether lifecycle state is ambiguous,
- why Qadam did or did not trade,
- whether the dashboard mirror is fresh.

Dashboard anti-slop rule:

```text
One state, one card, one reason, one next action.
```

Example:

```text
PaperOps readiness: held
Reason: scanner fresh, but candidate identity duplicated existing XLE setup.
Next: wait for a distinct source packet or invalidation window.
```

### A.15 Phase 0 Certification Gates

Phase 0 certification should run these gates before QSASE claims PaperOps
handoff readiness:

```text
source gap check
scanner freshness check
candidate identity check
review signature schema check
quantum review schema check
risk review check
paper-only broker-write guard
idempotency and duplicate exposure guard
pending lifecycle explanation check
proof lineage check
telemetry consistency check
Alpaca paper mirror check
dashboard preflight
canonical PaperOps pass
```

Certification output:

```text
phase0_status
scanner_freshness_status
fresh_candidate_identity_status
lifecycle_explanation_status
edge_graduation_status
proof_lineage_status
telemetry_consistency_status
deploy_hygiene_status
review_signature_status
paperops_route_status
canonical_paperops_pass_status
blockers
gaps
next_required_action
```

The final Phase 0 end state is:

```text
Qadam's PaperOps substrate is reliable enough for QSASE to depend on it. Qadam
may route distinct qualified candidates to guarded PaperOps review, and guarded
PaperOps may submit paper orders when all existing gates pass.
```

The final Phase 0 end state is not:

```text
QSASE can submit orders directly, enable live capital, bypass PaperOps, treat
LLM or quantum output as broker authority, or grant paper proof ledger credit
from backtests.
```

### A.16 Phase 0 Summary

The old plan remains relevant because it names the operational failure modes
that could make QSASE look weaker than it is:

- stale scanning,
- duplicate-shaped candidates,
- ambiguous pending fills,
- ungraduated edge records,
- broken proof lineage,
- contradictory telemetry,
- stale dashboard deploys,
- unclear review signatures.

QSASE should learn from this by treating PaperOps reliability as Phase 0.

The 24-part plan explains how Qadam evolves into a self-aware strategy engine.
This appendix explains how Qadam proves the paper-operation substrate is strong
enough for that evolution to matter in real guarded paper trading.
